Result: EffOPD在无需额外可训练模块或复杂超参数调优的情况下，实现了平均3倍的训练加速，同时保持了与原方法相当的最终性能。

Conclusion: OPD的高效性源于其在训练初期就形成的稳定更新轨迹，这种‘预判’机制为设计更高效的后训练方法提供了新思路，EffOPD验证了该视角的实用性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an efficient post-training paradigm for large language models. However, existing studies largely attribute this advantage to denser and more stable supervision, while the parameter-level mechanisms underlying OPD's efficiency remain poorly understood. In this work, we argue that OPD's efficiency stems from a form of ``foresight'': it establishes a stable update trajectory toward the final model early in training. This foresight manifests in two aspects. First, at the \textbf{Module-Allocation Level}, OPD identifies regions with low marginal utility and concentrates updates on modules that are more critical to reasoning. Second, at the \textbf{Update-Direction Level}, OPD exhibits stronger low-rank concentration, with its dominant subspaces aligning closely with the final update subspace early in training. Building on these findings, we propose \textbf{EffOPD}, a plug-and-play acceleration method that speeds up OPD by adaptively selecting an extrapolation step size and moving along the current update direction. EffOPD requires no additional trainable modules or complex hyperparameter tuning, and achieves an average training acceleration of $3\times$ while maintaining comparable final performance. Overall, our findings provide a parameter-dynamics perspective for understanding the efficiency of OPD and offer practical insights for designing more efficient post-training methods for large language models.

</details>


### [74] [Training-Inference Consistent Segmented Execution for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11744)
*Xianpeng Shang,Jiang Li,Zehua Duo,Qianyi Cai,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种训练与推理一致的分段生成框架，通过限制梯度传播范围，在保持性能的同时显著提升长上下文场景下的可扩展性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有高效长上下文方法在训练与推理之间存在执行不一致的问题，尤其是全上下文注意力带来的计算与内存开销。

Method: 采用分段级生成框架，训练和推理均遵循相同的分段前向执行语义；训练时仅允许从上一分段传递的KV状态参与梯度传播，头部可访问历史KV状态但不参与反向传播。

Result: 在多个长上下文基准上性能媲美全上下文注意力方法，相比FlashAttention在128K长度下峰值预填充内存降低约6倍，具备良好的延迟-内存权衡。

Conclusion: 该训练-推理一致的分段生成框架有效缓解了长上下文生成中的可扩展性瓶颈，为高效长上下文建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models face severe scalability challenges in long-context generation due to the computational and memory costs of full-context attention. Under practical computation and memory constraints, many inference-efficient long-context methods improve efficiency by adopting bounded-context or segment-level execution only during inference, while continuing to train models under full-context attention, resulting in a mismatch between training and inference execution and state-transition semantics. Based on this insight, we propose a training-inference consistent segment-level generation framework, in which training and inference follow the same segment-level forward execution semantics. During training, consistency with inference is enforced by restricting gradient propagation to KV states carried over from the immediately preceding segment, while permitting head-specific access to past KV states during the forward pass without involving them in gradient propagation. Across long-context benchmarks, our approach achieves performance comparable to full-context attention, while achieving competitive latency-memory trade-offs against strong inference-efficient baselines, and substantially improving scalability at very long context lengths (e.g., approximately 6x lower peak prefill memory at 128K compared to full-context attention with FlashAttention).

</details>


### [75] [Safety-Oriented Evaluation of Language Understanding Systems for Air Traffic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11769)
*Yujing Chang,Yash Guleria,Duc-Thinh Pham,Nhut-Huy Pham,Ningli Wang,Vu N. Duong,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向航空交通管制（ATC）场景的安全导向、后果感知的LLM评估框架，发现当前大模型在高风险语义错误上存在严重缺陷，尽管宏观指标较高，但操作可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法使用均一化指标（如F1、宏准确率）无法反映ATC领域中不同错误的不对称风险，尤其是高风险语义错误可能导致严重后果，因此需要一种更贴近安全关键场景的评估方式。

Method: 设计了一种针对ATC操作的后果感知评估框架，引入风险评分（Risk Score）以量化模型在关键语义元素（如跑道编号、移动限制）上的错误影响，并在真实转录数据上评估多个LLM的表现。

Result: 当前LLMs在清洁转录文本上最高风险评分为0.69，多数模型低于0.6，且高影响实体识别错误集中，显示出结构化理解能力不足，而动作类型分类相对稳定。

Conclusion: 在安全关键的ATC环境中，传统聚合指标不足以衡量LLM的可靠性，必须采用考虑错误后果的评估协议，以确保AI辅助系统的负责任部署。

Abstract: Air Traffic Control (ATC) is a safety-critical domain in which incorrect interpretation of instructions may lead to severe operational consequences. While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong general performance, their reliability in operational ATC environments remains unclear. Existing evaluation approaches, largely based on aggregate metrics such as F1 or macro accuracy, treat all errors uniformly and fail to account for the asymmetric consequences of high-risk semantic mistakes (e.g., incorrect runway identifiers or movement constraints). To address this gap, we propose a safety-oriented, consequence-aware evaluation framework tailored to ATC operations. Our results reveal that while current LLMs achieve reasonable aggregate accuracy, their operational reliability is severely limited. Evaluated on clean transcripts, the peak Risk Score reaches only 0.69, with most models scoring below 0.6 despite high macro-F1 performance. Further analysis shows that errors concentrate in high-impact entities despite relatively stable action-type classification, indicating structural grounding deficiencies. These findings highlight the necessity of consequence-aware evaluation protocols for the responsible deployment of AI-assisted ATC systems.

</details>


### [76] [From Token to Token Pair: Efficient Prompt Compression for Large Language Models in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11774)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Zhiyao Luo,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MedTPE的分层方法，用于对电子健康记录（EHR）序列进行无损压缩，通过合并频繁共现的医学词元对来减少输入长度和推理延迟，同时保持甚至提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EHR序列压缩方法要么增加额外模块导致推理延迟，要么可能丢失重要临床信息，因此需要一种无损且不增加成本的压缩方法。

Method: MedTPE扩展了标准的EHR序列分词方法，采用依赖感知的替换策略，将频繁共现的医学词元对合并为复合词元，并仅微调新增词元（占LLM参数0.5-1.0%）的嵌入。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，MedTPE最多可将输入词元长度减少31%，推理延迟降低34-63%，并在多个LLM和四项临床预测任务中保持或提升预测性能和输出格式合规性。

Conclusion: MedTPE是一种高效、无损的EHR序列压缩方法，具有良好的跨模型、跨领域和跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: By processing electronic health records (EHRs) as natural language sequences, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential in clinical prediction tasks such as mortality prediction and phenotyping. However, longitudinal or highly frequent EHRs often yield excessively long token sequences that result in high computational costs and even reduced performance. Existing solutions either add modules for compression or remove less important tokens, which introduce additional inference latency or risk losing clinical information. To achieve lossless compression of token sequences without additional cost or loss of performance, we propose Medical Token-Pair Encoding (MedTPE), a layered method that extends standard tokenisation for EHR sequences. MedTPE merges frequently co-occurring medical token pairs into composite tokens, providing lossless compression while preserving the computational complexity through a dependency-aware replacement strategy. Only the embeddings of the newly introduced tokens of merely 0.5-1.0% of the LLM's parameters are fine-tuned via self-supervised learning. Experiments on real-world datasets for two clinical scenarios demonstrate that MedTPE reduces input token length by up to 31% and inference latency by 34-63%, while maintaining or even improving both predictive performance and output format compliance across multiple LLMs and four clinical prediction tasks. Furthermore, MedTPE demonstrates robustness across different input context lengths and generalisability to scientific and financial domains and different languages.

</details>


### [77] [Choosing features for classifying multiword expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11779)
*Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种增强的多词表达式（MWE）分类方法，综合考虑多种语言特征以提高跨语言适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多词表达式具有异质性，且现有分类方法在计算应用中的效果参差不齐，因此需要一种更可靠、更适合计算使用的分类体系。

Method: 基于已有跨语言研究，选取能更可靠地将MWE分配到类别的特征，构建增强型分类框架。

Result: 提出一个更具跨语言适用性的MWE增强分类方案，强调特征选择对分类实用性的影响。

Conclusion: 合理的特征选择能够提升MWE分类在计算语言学中的有效性与多语言适应性。

Abstract: Multiword expressions (MWEs) are a heterogeneous set with a glaring need for classifications. Designing a satisfactory classification involves choosing features. In the case of MWEs, many features are a priori available. Not all features are equal in terms of how reliably MWEs can be assigned to classes. Accordingly, resulting classifications may be more or less fruitful for computational use. I outline an enhanced classification. In order to increase its suitability for many languages, I use previous works taking into account various languages.

</details>


### [78] [Probabilistic Calibration Is a Trainable Capability in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11845)
*Davide Baldelli,Sruthi Kuriakose,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Amal Zouaq,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过微调语言模型以满足用户指定的随机性约束，研究了软目标和硬目标两种校准微调方法，发现两者均能显著提升结构化采样的保真度，但在不同任务上表现各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在输出需要满足特定随机性约束的应用中表现不佳，其生成概率与目标常不匹配，因此探索是否可以通过微调来改善这一能力。

Method: 在合成提示数据上对语言模型进行微调，比较两种校准微调方法：软目标方法将目标分布转化为基于trie的下一个词预测目标，硬目标方法则使用从目标分布中采样的完成结果进行训练。

Result: 在12个跨四个家族的模型上，两种方法均显著提升了在未见分布族和参数设置上的结构化采样保真度；硬目标在数值采样任务上表现更优，软目标在开放随机生成、多选题平衡和NoveltyBench等任务上更好，但微调可能带来下游能力（如算术推理）的下降。

Conclusion: 概率校准是一种可通过微调训练获得的能力，硬目标配置更适合精确数值保真，软目标配置更适合广泛的随机生成迁移。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in settings where outputs must satisfy user-specified randomness constraints, yet their generation probabilities are often poorly calibrated to those targets. We study whether this capability can be improved directly through fine-tuning. Concretely, we fine-tune language models on synthetic prompts that require sampling from mathematical distributions, and compare two Calibration Fine-Tuning variants: a soft-target method that converts the desired output distribution into trie-derived next-token targets, and a hard-target method that trains on sampled completions from the same target distribution. Across 12 models spanning four families, both methods substantially improve structured-sampling fidelity on held-out distribution families and unseen parameter settings, showing that probabilistic calibration is a trainable capability. Under our selected training configurations, the two methods exhibit different empirical profiles: hard-target fine-tuning is often strongest on structured numeric sampling, while soft-target fine-tuning performs better on broader stochastic generation benchmarks, including open-ended random generation, multiple-choice answer-position balancing, and NoveltyBench. The gains sometimes reduce downstream capability, especially arithmetic reasoning, with costs varying by model. Overall, our results show that probabilistic calibration can be improved through fine-tuning, with our hard-target configuration favoring exact numeric fidelity and our soft-target configuration favoring broader stochastic transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/chandar-lab/calibration-finetuning.

</details>


### [79] [Self-Distilled Trajectory-Aware Boltzmann Modeling: Bridging the Training-Inference Discrepancy in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11854)
*Kecheng Chen,Ziru Liu,Xijia Tao,Hui Liu,Yibing Liu,Xinyu Fu,Shi Wu,Suiyun Zhang,Dandan Tu,Lingpeng Kong,Rui Liu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自蒸馏轨迹的后训练框架TABOM，通过玻尔兹曼建模对齐扩散语言模型训练与推理过程中的“由易到难”结构，显著提升模型在新领域的性能并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型在标准NELBO目标下进行后训练时，未能有效利用推理过程中的多步去噪轨迹，导致训练与推理不一致，限制了模型能力的提升。

Method: 提出TABOM框架，将推理过程中的去噪偏好建模为预测熵上的玻尔兹曼分布，并设计可追踪的成对排序目标，使模型的置信度顺序与实际解码轨迹对齐。

Result: TABOM在多个新领域上显著优于标准微调方法，扩展了扩散语言模型的有效知识边界，并大幅缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏轨迹不仅可用于加速推理，还能用于真正的知识获取；TABOM通过优化对齐训练与推理路径，提升了扩散语言模型的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, offering stronger global awareness and highly parallel generation. However, post-training DLMs with standard Negative Evidence Lower Bound (NELBO)-based supervised fine-tuning remains inefficient: training reconstructs randomly masked tokens in a single step, whereas inference follows a confidence-guided, multi-step easy-to-hard denoising trajectory. Recent trajectory-based self-distillation methods exploit such inference trajectories mainly for sampling-step compression and acceleration, often improving decoding efficiency without substantially enhancing the model's underlying capability, and may even degrade performance under full diffusion decoding. In this work, we ask whether self-distilled trajectories can be used not merely for faster inference, but for genuine knowledge acquisition. Although these trajectories lie on the pretrained DLM's own distributional manifold and thus offer a potentially lower optimization barrier, we find that naively fine-tuning on them with standard NELBO objectives yields only marginal gains. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{T}rajectory-\textbf{A}ligned optimization via \textbf{Bo}ltzmann \textbf{M}odeling (\textbf{TABOM}), a self-distilled trajectory-based post-training framework that aligns training with the easy-to-hard structure of inference. TABOM models the inference unmasking preference as a Boltzmann distribution over predictive entropies and derives a tractable pairwise ranking objective to align the model's certainty ordering with the observed decoding trajectory. Empirically, TABOM achieves substantial gains in new domains, expands the effective knowledge boundary of DLMs, and significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting compared with standard SFT.

</details>


### [80] [Concordance Comparison as a Means of Assembling Local Grammars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11862)
*Juliana Pirovani,Elias de Oliveira,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过比较局部语法（LG）的共现结果来优化人名识别的方法，并在葡萄牙语文本中取得了比现有技术高6个百分点的F值提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高葡萄牙语文本中人名识别的准确率，需要选择最优的局部语法（LG）组合。

Method: 使用工具比较不同局部语法生成的共现结果，分析它们之间的包含、交集和互斥关系，从而选出表现最佳的LG组合，并应用于HAREM第二阶段的金标准数据集。

Result: 在HAREM金标准数据集上达到76.86的F值，相比当前最佳结果提升了6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过比较和分析局部语法的输出差异，能够有效提升命名实体识别性能，尤其适用于葡萄牙语的人名识别任务。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition for person names is an important but non-trivial task in information extraction. This article uses a tool that compares the concordances obtained from two local grammars (LG) and highlights the differences. We used the results as an aid to select the best of a set of LGs. By analyzing the comparisons, we observed relationships of inclusion, intersection and disjunction within each pair of LGs, which helped us to assemble those that yielded the best results. This approach was used in a case study on extraction of person names from texts written in Portuguese. We applied the enhanced grammar to the Gold Collection of the Second HAREM. The F-Measure obtained was 76.86, representing a gain of 6 points in relation to the state-of-the-art for Portuguese.

</details>


### [81] [Qwen-Scope: Turning Sparse Features into Development Tools for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11887)
*Boyi Deng,Xu Wang,Yaoning Wang,Yu Wan,Yubo Ma,Baosong Yang,Haoran Wei,Jialong Tang,Huan Lin,Ruize Gao,Tianhao Li,Qian Cao,Xuancheng Ren,Xiaodong Deng,An Yang,Fei Huang,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Qwen-Scope，一个基于Qwen模型系列的稀疏自编码器（SAE）开源工具集，展示了SAE不仅可用于大语言模型的事后分析，还能作为接口在推理控制、评估分析、数据工作流和后训练优化四个方面推动模型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能力强大，但其内部决策过程不透明，限制了对其的检查、控制和系统性改进。因此需要发展机械可解释性研究，以增强对模型行为的理解与调控。

Method: 构建了名为Qwen-Scope的开源SAE套件，涵盖Qwen3和Qwen3.5系列中的7种模型变体、14组SAE，支持密集型和混合专家架构，并探索SAE在四个实际应用方向的作用：推理时引导、评估分析、数据中心化工作流和后训练优化。

Result: 实验证明SAE不仅能实现模型激活的稀疏可解释分解，还可作为实用接口用于控制语言风格、概念偏好、检测基准冗余、多语言毒性分类、安全数据生成，并通过引入监督微调和强化学习目标来减少代码切换和重复等不良行为。

Conclusion: SAE不仅是分析工具，更可作为连接模型内部机制与下游行为的可重用接口；开源Qwen-Scope旨在促进机制性研究并加速模型开发的实际工作流。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet their internal decision-making processes remain largely opaque, limiting our ability to inspect, control, and systematically improve them. This opacity motivates a growing body of research in mechanistic interpretability, with sparse autoencoders (SAEs) emerging as one of the most promising tools for decomposing model activations into sparse, interpretable feature representations. We introduce Qwen-Scope, an open-source suite of SAEs built on the Qwen model family, comprising 14 groups of SAEs across 7 model variants from the Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 series, covering both dense and mixture-of-expert architectures. Built on top of these SAEs, we show that SAEs can go beyond post-hoc analysis to serve as practical interfaces for model development along four directions: (i) inference-time steering, where SAE feature directions control language, concepts, and preferences without modifying model weights; (ii) evaluation analysis, where activated SAE features provide a representation-level proxy for benchmark redundancy and capability coverage; (iii) data-centric workflows, where SAE features support multilingual toxicity classification and safety-oriented data synthesis; and (iv) post-training optimization, where SAE-derived signals are incorporated into supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning objectives to mitigate undesirable behaviors such as code-switching and repetition. Together, these results demonstrate that SAEs can serve not only as post-hoc analysis tools, but also as reusable representation-level interfaces for diagnosing, controlling, evaluating, and improving large language models. By open-sourcing Qwen-Scope, we aim to support mechanistic research and accelerate practical workflows that connect model internals to downstream behavior.

</details>


### [82] [Enhancing Target-Guided Proactive Dialogue Systems via Conversational Scenario Modeling and Intent-Keyword Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11964)
*Maodong Li,Yancui Li,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过联合建模对话场景和意图关键词桥接来提升目标导向型主动对话系统性能的方法，显著提高了系统的主动性、流畅性和信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作忽略了对话场景和意图关键词的动态建模，导致与真实对话动态不匹配。

Method: 将用户画像和领域知识联合建模为对话场景，并引入意图关键词桥接机制以预测下一轮对话的意图关键词。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中均显示出对话场景建模和意图关键词桥接的有效性，显著提升了系统表现。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效缩小了目标导向型主动对话系统与真实世界交互之间的差距。

Abstract: A target-guided proactive dialogue system aims to steer conversations proactively toward pre-defined targets, such as designated keywords or specific topics. During guided conversations, dynamically modeling conversational scenarios and intent keywords to guide system utterance generation is beneficial; however, existing work largely overlooks this aspect, resulting in a mismatch with the dynamics of real-world conversations. In this paper, we jointly model user profiles and domain knowledge as conversational scenarios to introduce a scenario bias that dynamically influences system utterances, and employ intent-keyword bridging to predict intent keywords for upcoming dialogue turns, providing higher level and more flexible guidance. Extensive automatic and human evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of conversational scenario modeling and intent keyword bridging, yielding substantial improvements in proactivity, fluency, and informativeness for target-guided proactive dialogue systems, thereby narrowing the gap with real world interactions.

</details>


### [83] [On Predicting the Post-training Potential of Pre-trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11978)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Kexin Yang,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Wenie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RuDE的新框架，用于预测大语言模型在后训练阶段的潜力，通过基于4C分类法构建的对比对实现对基础模型能力的有效评估，并验证了其与后训练性能的高度相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试（如MMLU）无法准确反映基础模型在复杂开放场景中的可塑性，导致模型选择效率低下。因此需要一种能更准确预测模型后训练潜力的方法。

Method: 提出了RuDE（基于评分标准的判别性评估）框架，利用响应判别来绕过基础模型的生成差距；根据4C分类法构建跨领域的精细对照对比对，以评估模型在不同维度上的表现差异。

Result: 实验显示RuDE与后训练性能的相关性超过90%；通过强化学习验证，RuDE能够有效识别出具有高潜力的小型模型，这些模型在后训练后表现优于更大的模型。

Conclusion: RuDE提供了一种计算高效的机制，用于评估和选择具有高后训练潜力的基础模型，有助于提升基础模型开发的效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: The performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) on downstream tasks is fundamentally constrained by the capabilities acquired during pre-training. However, traditional benchmarks like MMLU often fail to reflect a base model's plasticity in complex open-ended scenarios, leading to inefficient model selection. We address this by introducing a new task of predicting post-training potential - forecasting a base model's performance before post-training. We propose RuDE (Rubric-based Discriminative Evaluation), a unified framework that bypasses the generation gap of base models by leveraging response discrimination. Guided by our systematic 4C Taxonomy, RuDE constructs controlled contrastive pairs across diverse domains by fine-grained rubric violations. Extensive experiments demonstrate a correlation greater than 90% with post-training performance. Crucially, validation via Reinforcement Learning (RL) confirms that RuDE effectively identifies high-potential smaller models that outperform larger counterparts, offering a compute-efficient mechanism for foundation model development.

</details>


### [84] [Towards Visually-Guided Movie Subtitle Translation for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11993)
*Tarun Chintada,Kshetrimayum Boynao Singh,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了电影字幕翻译中的多模态问题，提出两种轻量级视觉辅助策略，并发现选择性视觉增强能有效提升低资源印度语言的翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有文本系统在处理低资源印度语言时难以捕捉情感、动作和社会细微差别，因此需要结合视觉信息来改善字幕翻译。

Method: 采用五部完整电影作为案例研究，比较基于5分钟滑动窗口的结构化属性摘要和字幕间视觉间隙的自由文本摘要两种视觉辅助策略，并引入选择性视觉增强方法。

Result: 发现字幕与画面之间的时间错位是主要障碍，盲目使用视觉信息效果有限；但选择性地对质量最低的20-30%片段进行视觉增强可显著提升COMET得分，且更节省计算资源；其中基于属性的粗略摘要更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在长视频字幕翻译中，应采用选择性而非全量视觉接地，尤其是使用结构化属性总结能更有效地捕捉仅文本无法表达的情境和情感线索。

Abstract: Movie subtitle translation is inherently multimodal, yet text-only systems often miss visual cues needed to convey emotion, action, and social nuance, especially for low-resource Indic languages (English to Hindi, Bengali, Telugu, Tamil and Kannada). We present a case study on five full-length films and compare two lightweight visual grounding strategies: structured attribute summaries from a 5-minute sliding window and free-text summaries of inter-subtitle visual gaps. Our analysis shows that temporal misalignment between subtitles and frames is a major obstacle in long-form video, often rendering indiscriminate visual grounding ineffective. However, oracle selective grounding, which replaces only the lowest-quality 20-30\% of baseline segments with visual-enhanced outputs, consistently improves COMET over the text-only baseline while requiring far less visual processing. Among the two approaches, coarse attribute-based visual context summarization is more robust, capturing scene-level emotion and contextual subtle cues that text alone often misses

</details>


### [85] [SAGE: Scalable Automated Robustness Augmentation for LLM Knowledge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12022)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yuzhe Wang,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Rui Men,Yichang Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE是一个可扩展的自动化生成框架，用于增强知识评估基准的鲁棒性，利用微调的小模型实现低成本、高质量的变体生成与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在标准知识评估基准上表现良好，但在面对测试相同知识的不同形式问题时仍显脆弱，且当前的生成-验证流程成本高、难以扩展。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，包括基于评分标准的验证器VariantQual和使用监督微调与强化学习优化的变体生成器VariantGen，利用小模型进行高效训练与迁移。

Result: 在HellaSwag上实验表明，SAGE能以显著更低的成本构建大规模、高质量的鲁棒性增强基准，并在MMLU等其他基准上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAGE实现了知识评估基准的高效、可扩展鲁棒性增强，为未来评估大模型知识能力提供了更具挑战性和多样性的测试环境。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on standard knowledge evaluation benchmarks, yet recent work shows that their knowledge capabilities remain brittle under question variants that test the same knowledge in different forms. Robustness augmentation of existing knowledge evaluation benchmarks is therefore necessary, but current LLM-assisted generate-then-verify pipelines are costly and difficult to scale due to low-yield variant generation and unreliable variant verification. We propose SAGE (Scalable Automated Generation of Robustness BEnchmarks), a framework for scalable robustness augmentation of knowledge evaluation benchmarks using fine-tuned smaller models. SAGE consists of VariantQual, a rubric-based verifier trained on human-labeled seed data, and VariantGen, a variant generator initialized with supervised fine-tuning and further optimized with reinforcement learning using VariantQual as the reward model. Experiments on HellaSwag show that SAGE constructs a large-scale robustness-augmented benchmark with quality comparable to the human-annotated HellaSwag-Pro at substantially lower cost, while the fine-tuned models further generalize to MMLU without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [86] [Caraman at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Three-Stage Multi-Turn Retrieval with Query Rewriting, Hybrid Search, and Cross-Encoder Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12028)
*David-Maximilian Caraman,Gheorghe Cosmin Silaghi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于SemEval-2026 Task 8的三阶段检索系统，在四个英文领域中实现了优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升多轮问答场景下跨领域的信息检索效果，解决上下文依赖问题。

Method: 采用LoRA微调Qwen 2.5 7B进行查询重写，结合BM25与稠密检索，并通过Reciprocal Rank Fusion融合结果，最后使用BGE-reranker-v2-m3进行重排序。

Result: 在官方测试集上达到nDCG@5为0.531，排名第八，比组织方基线高出10.7%；领域特定的温度调优策略带来持续增益。

Conclusion: 三阶段混合检索流程有效，且查询重写的解码策略需根据领域特性调整，复杂方法如多查询扩展反而可能降低性能。

Abstract: We describe our system for SemEval-2026 Task 8 (MTRAGEval), participating in Task A (Retrieval) across four English-language domains. Our approach employs a three-stage pipeline: (1) query rewriting via a LoRA-fine-tuned Qwen 2.5 7B model that transforms context-dependent follow-up questions into standalone queries, (2) hybrid BM25 and dense retrieval combined through Reciprocal Rank Fusion, and (3) cross-encoder reranking with BGE-reranker-v2-m3. On the official test set, the system achieves nDCG@5 of 0.531, ranking 8th out of 38 participating systems and 10.7% above the organizer baseline. Development comparisons reveal that domain-specific temperature tuning for query generation, where technical domains benefit from deterministic decoding and general domains from controlled randomness, provides consistent gains, while more complex strategies such as domain-aware prompting and multi-query expansion degrade performance.

</details>


### [87] [SkillGraph: Skill-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Agents via Evolving Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12039)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Yubo Ma,Wenjie Wang,Dayiheng Liu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKILLGRAPH是一种将可复用技能表示为有向图中节点的框架，通过类型化边编码先决、增强和共现关系，支持复杂任务中的多步决策，并在多个任务上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库大多将技能存储为孤立条目，仅基于语义相似性检索，难以应对需要组合多个技能的复杂任务，且缺乏结构信息以有效维护技能库。

Method: 提出SKILLGRAPH框架，将技能作为有向图的节点，利用带类型的边（如先决、增强、共现）建模技能间关系；基于智能体轨迹和强化学习反馈持续更新图结构，并检索有序的技能子图以指导多步决策。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和七个搜索增强型问答任务上的实验表明，SKILLGRAPH优于现有的记忆增强强化学习方法，尤其在需要多技能组合的复杂任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: SKILLGRAPH通过结构化的图表示有效提升了技能的重用性与组合能力，实现了技能库维护与策略学习的协同进化，显著提高了智能体在复杂任务中的性能。

Abstract: Skill libraries enable large language model agents to reuse experience from past interactions, but most existing libraries store skills as isolated entries and retrieve them only by semantic similarity. This leads to two key challenges for compositional tasks. Firstly, an agent must identify not only relevant skills but also how they depend on and build upon each other. Secondly, it also makes library maintenance difficult, since the system lacks structural cues for deciding when skills should be merged, split, or removed. We propose SKILLGRAPH, a framework that represents reusable skills as nodes in a directed graph, with typed edges encoding prerequisite, enhancement, and co-occurrence relations. Given a new task, SKILLGRAPH retrieves not just individual skills, but an ordered skill subgraph that can guide multi-step decision making. The graph is continuously updated from agent trajectories and reinforcement learning feedback, allowing both the skill library and the agent policy to improve together. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and seven search-augmented QA tasks show that SKILLGRAPH achieves state-of-the-art performance against memory-augmented RL methods, with especially large gains on complex tasks that require composing multiple skills.

</details>


### [88] [Is Child-Directed Language Optimized for Word Learning? A Computational Study of Verb Meaning Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12047)
*Francesca Padovani,Jaap Jumelet,Yevgen Matusevych,Arianna Bisazza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过神经语言模型比较儿童导向语言（CDL）和成人导向语言（ADL）对动词意义习得的影响，发现口语输入（尤其是CDL）中语义发展先于句法，且句法受损时学习更具韧性，但该优势可能源于口语体裁的普遍特性，而非CDL特有优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究儿童导向语言（CDL）是否为促进语言学习而优化，以及其具体支持哪些语言发展方面。

Method: 使用在CDL与ADL上训练的神经语言模型，选择性去除语法或词汇共现信息，并评估这些操作对动词意义习得的影响，同时追踪训练过程中的语义与句法表现。

Result: 破坏句法会损害所有数据集上的学习，但在CDL和口语ADL上训练的模型比书面输入更具韧性；训练过程中表现出语义优先的发展轨迹，动词意义在句法成熟前出现，这种异步性在口语尤其是CDL中最明显。

Conclusion: 动词学习的优势可能反映的是口语体裁的普遍特性，而非CDL特有的优化机制。

Abstract: Is child-directed language (CDL) optimized to support language learning, and which aspects of linguistic development does it facilitate? We investigate this question using neural language models trained on CDL versus adult-directed language (ADL). We selectively remove syntactic or lexical co-occurrence information from the model training data, and evaluate the impact of these manipulations on verb meaning acquisition. While disrupting syntax impairs learning across all datasets, models trained on CDL and spoken ADL show significantly higher resilience than those trained on written input. Tracking semantic and syntactic performance over training, we observe a semantic-first trajectory, with verb meanings emerging prior to robust syntactic proficiency, an asynchrony most pronounced in the spoken domain, especially CDL. These results suggest that the advantage for verb learning previously attributed to CDL may instead reflect broader properties of the spoken register, rather than a uniquely CDL-specific optimization.

</details>


### [89] [Do Language Models Encode Knowledge of Linguistic Constraint Violations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12055)
*Hardy,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）是否在其参数中编码了语言约束违规的表征。通过稀疏自编码器分解多义激活并提出敏感性评分和联合证伪框架进行检测，研究发现当前LM中缺乏统一的语法违规检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在语言任务上表现优异，但其内部如何处理语法错误尚不清楚。本文旨在探究LLM是否在参数中编码了对语法违规的表示，并试图识别与这些违规相关的神经特征。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器将模型中的多义激活分解为稀疏的单义特征，引入敏感性评分来识别对语法违规输入更敏感的特征，并设计了一个包含三个标准的联合证伪框架以共同评估这些特征的有效性。

Result: 结果总体呈负面：一是不同语言现象之间未能同时满足全部三个证伪标准；二是未发现跨所有类别一致共享的违规相关特征。尽管某些现象显示出部分选择性因果结构的证据，但整体支持较弱。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型缺乏统一且稳定的语法违规检测机制，未发现存在广泛共享的、专门用于识别语法错误的内部特征。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong linguistic performance, yet their internal mechanisms for producing these predictions remain unclear. We investigate the hypothesis that LLMs encode representations of linguistic constraint violations within their parameters, which are selectively activated when processing ungrammatical sentences. To test this, we use sparse autoencoders to decompose polysemantic activations into sparse, monosemantic features and recover candidates for violation-related features.
  We introduce a sensitivity score for identifying features that are preferentially activated on constraint-violated versus well-formed inputs, enabling unsupervised detection of potential violation-specific features. We further propose a conjunctive falsification framework with three criteria evaluated jointly.
  Overall, the results are negative in two respects: (1) the falsification criteria are not jointly satisfied across linguistic phenomena, and (2) no features are consistently shared across all categories. While some phenomena show partial evidence of selective causal structure, the overall pattern provides limited support for a unified set of grammatical violation detectors in current LMs.

</details>


### [90] [Sign Language Recognition and Translation for Low-Resource Languages: Challenges and Pathways Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12096)
*Nigar Alishzade,Gulchin Abdullayeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统回顾了针对资源匮乏手语（以阿塞拜疆手语为例）的识别与翻译研究，提出八项行动建议和三项范式转变，强调以数据为中心、 signer自适应系统及任务特定评估指标，并倡导基于社区协作的技术发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于记录有限、数据集稀疏和计算工具不足，全球300多种手语仍属低资源语言，亟需系统性研究支持其技术发展。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述，结合阿塞拜疆手语案例研究，分析全球倡议并提取可操作经验，特别关注突厥语系手语间的语言相近性以促进迁移学习。

Result: 提炼出八项关键教训，提出三大范式转变，并为阿塞拜疆手语制定基于MediaPipe轻量架构、社区验证标注和离线优先部署的技术路线图。

Conclusion: 手语技术的发展必须依托持续的跨学科合作，以聋人社区为中心，确保文化真实性、伦理治理和实际沟通价值。

Abstract: Sign languages are natural, visual-gestural languages used by Deaf communities worldwide. Over 300 distinct sign languages remain severely low-resource due to limited documentation, sparse datasets, and insufficient computational tools. This systematic review synthesizes literature on sign language recognition and translation for under-resourced languages, using Azerbaijan Sign Language (AzSL) as a case study. Analysis of global initiatives extracts eight actionable lessons, including community co-design, dialectal diversity capture, and privacy-preserving pose-based representations. Turkic sign languages (Kazakh, Turkish, Azerbaijani) receive special attention, as linguistic proximity enables effective transfer learning. We propose three paradigm shifts: from architecture-centric to data-centric AI, from signer-independent to signer-adaptive systems, and from reference-based to task-specific evaluation metrics. A technical roadmap for AzSL leverages lightweight MediaPipe-based architectures, community-validated annotations, and offline-first deployment. Progress requires sustained interdisciplinary collaboration centered on Deaf communities to ensure cultural authenticity, ethical governance, and practical communication benefit.

</details>


### [91] [Metaphor Is Not All Attention Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12128)
*Olga Sorokoletova,Francesco Giarrusso,Giacomo De Luca,Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Marcello Galisai,Vincenzo Suriani,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了文学性改写（如诗歌形式）如何绕过大型语言模型的安全机制，发现 jailbreak 的成功并非因为模型无法识别诗歌格式，而是由于风格上的不规则性改变了模型对提示的处理方式，使其避开训练中设定的词汇触发器。通过注意力模式的可解释性分析，作者发现模型能准确区分文学格式，但无法据此预测是否会被攻击成功，表明现有安全机制需考虑风格引发的行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨文学性 jailbreak 为何有效，尤其是诗歌等文体转换是否通过特定修辞手法或格式识别失败来突破安全防护，从而揭示当前安全训练的盲点。

Method: 采用注意力机制的可解释性分析方法，进行输入层面的消融实验以评估不同诗歌修辞的作用；构建注意力图的可解释向量表示，并对其进行聚类分析，同时训练线性探针预测安全结果和文学格式。

Result: 模型能够高精度区分诗歌与散文格式，但在同一格式内难以预测 jailbreak 是否成功；聚类结果显示按文学格式分离明显，但不按安全性标签分离；说明 jailbreak 成功不是因为未识别格式，而是风格异常导致处理路径改变。

Conclusion: 文学性 jailbreak 的有效性源于累积的风格不规则性，而非单一修辞或格式识别失败，这些变化影响了模型的提示处理机制并规避了后训练中的词汇触发检测。因此，提升鲁棒性需要引入能适应风格诱导行为变化的安全机制。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where their ability to resist harmful instructions is essential. Although post-training aims to make models robust against many jailbreak strategies, recent evidence shows that stylistic reformulations, such as poetic transformation, can still bypass safety mechanisms with alarming effectiveness. This raises a central question: why do literary jailbreaks succeed? In this work, we investigate whether their effectiveness depends on specific poetic devices, on a failure to recognize literary formatting, or on deeper changes in how models process stylistically irregular prompts. We address this problem through an interpretability analysis of attention patterns. We perform input-level ablation studies to assess the contribution of individual and combinations of poetic devices; construct an interpretable vector representation of attention maps; cluster these representations and train linear probes to predict safety outcomes and literary format. Our results show that models distinguish poetic from prose formats with high accuracy, yet struggle to predict jailbreak success within each format. Clustering further reveals clear separation by literary format, but not by safety label. These findings indicate that jailbreak success is not caused by a failure to recognize poetic formatting; rather, poetic prompts induce distinct processing patterns that remain largely independent of harmful-content detection. Overall, literary jailbreaks appear to misalign large language models not through any single poetic device, but through accumulated stylistic irregularities that alter prompt processing and avoid lexical triggers considered during post-training. This suggests that robustness requires safety mechanisms that account for style-induced shifts in model behavior. We use Qwen3-14B as a representative open-weight case study.

</details>


### [92] [Latent Causal Void: Explicit Missing-Context Reconstruction for Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12156)
*Hui Li,Zhongquan Jian,Jinsong Su,Junfeng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“潜在因果空缺”（LCV）的检索引导检测器，通过显式重建每句话中缺失的事实，并将其用于图推理，提升了对因信息遗漏导致的误导性内容的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理因遗漏背景事实而导致的误导信息时，通常仅附加检索到的上下文或推断是否存在遗漏，而未显式建模具体缺失的内容。本文旨在通过明确重建缺失事实来提升检测性能。

Method: LCV首先检索时间对齐的上下文文章，利用冻结的大语言模型为每个句子-文章对生成简短的缺失上下文描述，并将这些关系文本作为边构建异质图，在目标句子与上下文文章之间进行图推理。

Result: 在Sheng等人提出的双语基准上，LCV相较于最强的遗漏感知基线模型，在英文和中文数据集上的macro-F1分别提升了2.56和2.84个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨源缺失事实本身比仅附加证据或预测遗漏信号更有利于遗漏型 misinformation 的检测。

Abstract: Automatic misinformation detection performs well when deception is visible in what an article explicitly states. However, some misinformation articles remain locally coherent and only become misleading once compared with contemporaneous reports that supply background facts the article omits. We study this omission-relevant setting and observe that current omission-aware approaches typically either attach retrieved context as auxiliary evidence or infer a categorical omission signal, leaving the specific missing fact implicit. We propose \emph{Latent Causal Void} (LCV), a retrieval-guided detector that explicitly reconstructs the missing fact for each target sentence and uses it as a textual cross-source relation in graph reasoning. Concretely, LCV retrieves temporally aligned context articles, asks a frozen instruction-tuned large language model to generate a short missing-context description for each sentence--article pair, and feeds the resulting relation text into a heterograph over target sentences and context articles. On the bilingual benchmark of Sheng et al., LCV improves over the strongest omission-aware baseline by $2.56$ and $2.84$ macro-F1 points on the English and Chinese splits, respectively. The results indicate that modeling the missing cross-source fact itself, rather than only attaching retrieved evidence or predicting an omission signal, is a useful representation for omission-aware misinformation detection.

</details>


### [93] [Mitigating Context-Memory Conflicts in LLMs through Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12185)
*Yigeng Zhou,Wu Li,Yifan Lu,Yequan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Wenya Wang,Jun Yu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为动态认知调和解码（DCRD）的两阶段解码方法，通过分析注意力图预测并缓解上下文与模型记忆间的知识冲突，在保持解码效率的同时提升了准确性，并构建了ConflictKG基准用于评估频繁知识更新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理知识冲突时，静态方法会破坏无冲突情况下的输出分布，而动态方法难以应对复杂现实场景，因此需要一种能自适应区分并处理冲突与非冲突情况的解码机制。

Method: DCRD首先通过分析注意力图评估上下文保真度以预测潜在冲突，随后根据预测结果选择两条解码路径之一：贪婪解码或基于上下文保真度的动态解码，从而实现对冲突的动态响应。

Result: 在四个大语言模型和六个问答数据集上的实验表明，DCRD优于所有基线方法，达到最先进的性能；同时构建了新的知识冲突问答基准ConflictKG。

Conclusion: DCRD能够有效识别并缓解参数化知识与外部上下文之间的知识冲突，在冲突存在时准确调和知识，在无冲突时保持高效解码，整体显著提升问答表现。

Abstract: Large language models accumulate extensive parametric knowledge through pre-training. However, knowledge conflicts occur when outdated or incorrect parametric knowledge conflicts with external knowledge in the context. Existing methods address knowledge conflicts through contrastive decoding, but in conflict-free scenarios, static approaches disrupt output distribution. Other dynamic decoding methods attempt to measure the degree of conflict but still struggle with complex real-world situations. In this paper, we propose a two-stage decoding method called Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding (DCRD), to predict and mitigate context-memory conflicts. DCRD first analyzes the attention map to assess context fidelity and predict potential conflicts. Based on this prediction, the input is directed to one of two decoding paths: (1) greedy decoding, or (2) context fidelity-based dynamic decoding. This design enables DCRD to handle conflicts efficiently while maintaining high accuracy and decoding efficiency in conflict-free cases. Additionally, to simulate scenarios with frequent knowledge updates, we constructed ConflictKG, a knowledge conflict QA benchmark. Experiments on four LLMs across six QA datasets show that DCRD outperforms all baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [94] [Mechanistic Interpretability of ASR models using Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12225)
*Dan Pluth,Zachary Nicholas Houghton,Yu Zhou,Vijay K. Gurbani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于基于Transformer的语音识别模型Whisper，揭示了其编码中丰富的语言信息，并发现了跨语言的单一语义特征和特征操控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度Transformer模型在多个影响公众领域的广泛应用，理解其内部机制变得至关重要。尽管已有研究将SAE用于文本大模型的可解释性分析，但尚无研究探索其在语音处理模型如ASR中的应用。

Method: 在Whisper模型的编码器提取的帧级嵌入上训练高维稀疏潜在空间的稀疏自编码器（SAE），以解析其内部表示。

Result: 发现了一系列跨越语言与非语言边界的单语义特征，并实现了跨语言的特征操控，表明Whisper编码了丰富的语言结构信息。

Conclusion: 验证了SAE在语音Transformer模型中进行机制解释的可行性，证明Whisper内部存在高度可解释的稀疏表示。

Abstract: Understanding the internal machinations of deep Transformer-based NLP models is more crucial than ever as these models see widespread use in various domains that affect the public at large, such as industry, academia, finance, health. While these models have advanced rapidly, their internal mechanisms remain largely a mystery. Techniques such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAE) have emerged to understand these mechanisms by projecting dense representations into a sparse vector. While existing research has demonstrated the viability of the SAE in interpreting text-based Large Language Models (LLMs), there are no equivalent studies that demonstrate the application of a SAE to audio processing models like Automatic Speech Recognizers (ASRs). In this work, a SAE is applied to Whisper, a Transformer-based ASR, training a high-dimensional sparse latent space on frame-level embeddings extracted from the Whisper encoder. Our work uncovers diverse monosemantic features across linguistic and non-linguistic boundaries, and demonstrates cross-lingual feature steering. This work establishes the viability of a SAE model and demonstrates that Whisper encodes a rich amount of linguistic information.

</details>


### [95] [Combining On-Policy Optimization and Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12227)
*Miguel Moura Ramos,Duarte M. Alves,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了dGRPO方法，结合了GRPO和OPD的优势，通过引入密集指导信号来提升大语言模型在长上下文任务中的稳定性与效果，并通过合成数据集LongBlocks验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文适配方法存在暴露偏差、训练不稳定或无法充分利用奖励信号等问题，需要一种更稳定且高效的方法来实现长上下文推理对齐。

Method: 提出Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization（dGRPO），将基于结果的策略优化（GRPO）与来自强教师模型的密集知识蒸馏（OPD）相结合，并构建合成数据集LongBlocks用于多跳推理、上下文定位和长文本生成任务的评估。

Result: 实验表明，dGRPO在长上下文任务中比纯SFT、KD或GRPO方法更稳定且性能更优，同时保持了短上下文任务的能力。

Conclusion: 结合结果导向的策略优化与知识蒸馏的密集指导，能有效提升大语言模型在长上下文场景下的推理能力，为长上下文对齐提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to long-context tasks requires post-training methods that remain accurate and coherent over thousands of tokens. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align training with model-generated states, but are unstable and sample-inefficient due to sparse rewards; 3) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance, but does not directly optimize arbitrary reward signals. In this paper, we propose Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO), a method for long-context reasoning that augments GRPO with dense guidance from a stronger teacher via OPD. We also introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic long-context dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. We conduct extensive experiments and ablations comparing off-policy training, sparse-reward GRPO, and our combined approach, leading to an improved recipe for long-context alignment. Overall, our results show that combining outcome-based policy optimization with knowledge distillation in a single objective provides a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning, while preserving short-context capabilities.

</details>


### [96] [Mind the Pause: Disfluency-Aware Objective Tuning for Multilingual Speech Correction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12242)
*Deepak Kumar,Baban Gain,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合序列标注、指令微调和对比学习的多语言语音识别转录纠错管道，有效提升非流利文本的流畅性和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动语音识别转录纠错方法多集中于检测或删除非流利词，易破坏语法结构和语义连贯性；而现有大模型研究未充分探索完整修正任务，缺乏对多语言场景的有效支持。

Method: 构建一个多语言纠错流程：首先使用序列标注器标记非流利词，利用这些标记信号指导大语言模型的指令微调进行文本重写，并引入对比学习目标，惩罚模型复制非流利词，从而增强语法保持与语义一致性。

Result: 在印地语、孟加拉语和马拉地语三种印度语言上的实验表明，该方法显著优于强基线模型（包括多语言序列到序列模型），验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 仅依赖检测策略不足以实现高质量的非流利文本纠正；结合词元级提示、指令微调与对比学习是一种实用且可扩展的多语言语音转录纠错解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts often contain disfluencies, such as fillers, repetitions, and false starts, which reduce readability and hinder downstream applications like chatbots and voice assistants. If left unaddressed, such disfluencies can significantly degrade the reliability of downstream systems. Most existing approaches rely on classical models that focus on identifying disfluent tokens for removal. While this strategy is effective to some extent, it often disrupts grammatical structure and semantic coherence, leading to incomplete or unnatural sentences. Recent literature explored the use of large language models (LLMs); however, these efforts have primarily focused on disfluency detection or data augmentation, rather than performing comprehensive correction. We propose a multilingual correction pipeline where a sequence tagger first marks disfluent tokens, and these signals guide instruction fine-tuning of an LLM to rewrite transcripts into fluent text. To further improve reliability, we add a contrastive learning objective that penalizes the reproduction of disfluent tokens, encouraging the model to preserve grammar and meaning while removing disfluent artifacts. Our experiments across three Indian languages, namely Hindi, Bengali, and Marathi show consistent improvements over strong baselines, including multilingual sequence-to-sequence models. These results highlight that detection-only strategies are insufficient. Combining token-level cues with instruction tuning and contrastive learning provides a practical and scalable solution for multilingual disfluency correction in speech-driven NLP systems. We make the codes publicly available at https://github.com/deepak-kumar-98/Mind-the-Pause.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种级联生成式框架，用于电商个性化 storefront 构建，通过生成页面区域主题和受限关键词来增强个性化与语义一致性，在线实验显示每页面浏览购物车添加量提升2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商首页由多个独立模块拼接而成，个性化和语义连贯性受限，难以适应动态目标和商品运营需求。

Method: 将首页构建分解为两个生成任务：区域级主题生成和受限关键词生成，并采用教师-学生模型微调以满足生产环境的延迟与成本约束，同时融合传统排序模型保持系统兼容性。

Result: 该方法在在线实验中相比强基线实现了每页面浏览购物车添加量+2.7%的提升，消融实验表明其性能接近闭源大模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的级联生成框架在保证系统稳定性与生成内容安全的前提下，显著提升了电商平台的动态个性化能力与商业效果。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [98] [EVOCHAMBER: Test-Time Co-evolution of Multi-Agent System at Individual, Team, and Population Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11136)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Shengyu Dai,Zhenwen Shao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOCHAMBER是一个无需训练的多智能体测试时进化框架，通过协同“梦游”机制实现跨智能体的非对称知识传递，在数学、代码和多领域推理任务中显著优于基线方法，并展现出自发的专业化分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时进化方法要么局限于单个智能体内部学习，无法实现跨智能体的知识共享，要么采用对称广播方式，破坏了协作中必要的专业化分工。本文旨在探索真正意义上的多智能体测试时进化机制。

Method: 提出EVOCHAMBER框架，包含三个层次的进化：基于CODREAM（协同梦游）协议的个体间非对称知识传递、在线组建任务条件化团队的团队级操作符，以及执行分叉、合并、剪枝和生成的群体生命周期管理。当团队失败或意见分歧时触发CODREAM，强智能体向弱智能体定向传递特定任务领域的知识。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上，EVOCHAMBER在竞赛数学、代码和多领域推理任务上分别达到63.9%、75.7%和87.1%的性能，数学任务相对提升32%，消融实验验证非对称跨智能体传输是主要驱动力。多个同源初始化智能体自发演化出4-5个稳定的专业化分支。

Conclusion: 多智能体测试时进化不仅仅是N次单智能体进化的简单重复，其核心在于协同结构、知识流动和群体动态的联合演化，EVOCHAMBER通过保留专业化的非对称知识传递机制，实现了更高效的集体学习与适应。

Abstract: We argue that multi-agent test-time evolution is not single-agent evolution replicated N times. A single-agent learner can only evolve its own context and memory. A multi-agent system additionally evolves who collaborates, how they collaborate, and how knowledge flows across the population. These components have no single-agent counterpart and can produce phenomena such as emergent specialization. Yet prior test-time methods either confine experiences to individual agents, forfeiting cross-agent learning, or broadcast symmetrically to all agents, erasing the specialization that makes collaboration valuable. We present EVOCHAMBER, a training-free framework that instantiates test-time evolution at three levels over a coevolving agent pool. At its core is CODREAM (Collaborative Dreaming), a post-task protocol triggered on team failure or disagreement, in which agents collaboratively reflect, distill insights, and route them asymmetrically from strong to weak agents on the failed niche, preserving specialization while filling knowledge gaps. Team-level operators assemble niche-conditioned teams and select collaboration structures online. Population-level lifecycle operators fork, merge, prune, and seed agents under performance pressure. On three heterogeneous task streams with Qwen3-8B, EVOCHAMBER reaches 63.9% on competition math, 75.7% on code, and 87.1% on multi-domain reasoning, outperforming the best baseline by 32% relative on math and confirming asymmetric cross-agent transfer as the primary driver in ablation. Starting from several identically initialized agents, four to five stable niche specialists spontaneously emerge, a structural signature of multi-agent evolution that no single-agent learner can express. See our code at: https://github.com/Mercury7353/EvoChamber

</details>


### [99] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankQ是一种用于离线到在线强化学习的Q-learning目标，通过引入自监督的多项目排序损失来增强时序差分学习，从而在稀疏奖励和视觉机器人任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大规模状态-动作空间中有限数据覆盖时，容易因价值函数过估计而导致次优策略；同时，对未见动作的统一惩罚会限制在线阶段的策略改进。

Method: 提出RankQ，结合时序差分学习与自监督的多项目排名损失，学习动作间的相对偏好，形成结构化的动作排序，引导Q函数梯度朝向更优行为。

Result: 在D4RL稀疏奖励基准上表现优于或媲美七种已有方法；在视觉机器人任务中，低数据下平均仿真成功率比次优方法高42.7%，高数据下提升13.7%，并实现强sim-to-real迁移，真实世界方块堆叠成功率从43.1%提升至84.7%。

Conclusion: RankQ通过学习相对动作偏好而非简单惩罚未见动作，有效缓解了离线到在线转换中的策略退化问题，在多种环境下均展现出优越的样本效率和策略提升能力。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [100] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型的在线蒸馏（OPD）和自蒸馏（OPSD）方法进行了系统性实证研究，揭示了其在不同场景下的有效性与失效机制，并提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对OP(S)D的效果结论不一，部分显示其在系统提示和知识内化方面有效，但也存在训练不稳定或性能下降的问题。因此需要明确OP(S)D何时有效、何时失败及其原因。

Method: 通过大规模实证分析，考察教师模型选择、损失函数设计、特权信息（PI）类型等因素对OPD和OPSD效果的影响，并识别导致失败的关键机制。

Result: 发现OPD在数学推理中对教师选择和损失函数敏感；OPSD在实例特定特权信息下失效，但在共享潜在规则（如系统提示）下有效；识别出三种失败机制：师生分布不匹配、优化不稳定梯度、学生学习无PI策略。引入stop-gradient TopK目标等方法可缓解这些问题。

Conclusion: OP(S)D的效果高度依赖于任务结构与训练设置，需根据特权信息的性质谨慎设计蒸馏策略，所提出的改进方法能显著提升稳定性与性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [101] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 嵌入图像中的数字锚点会系统性地影响视觉-语言模型的质量判断，且这种偏差与模型架构相关的表征动态密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉-语言模型在质量评估任务中是否受到嵌入数字锚点的影响，并揭示其背后的表征机制。

Method: 在六个来自五种不同架构的视觉-语言模型上分析数字锚点效应，通过层间探测和融合分析研究表征动态与行为偏差的关系。

Result: 发现数字锚点显著扭曲质量判断（eta² = 0.18–0.77, p < 0.001），其效应是严重图像质量下降的2.5倍；锚点分类在较浅层（L12-L34）饱和，而最优质量预测位于更深的层；模型间的融合模式存在架构依赖性差异。

Conclusion: 数字锚点偏差具有因果性，源于多模态信息融合方式和层级表征动态的相互作用，提示需改进融合机制以提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLATIM，一个用于评估视觉-语言模型在经典物理益智游戏《The Incredible Machine 2》中人类级逻辑推理能力的基准，揭示了现有模型在高阶推理与精确视觉定位执行之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型基准多忽视交互环境中所需的复杂物理推理能力，尤其在需要精细鼠标操作的点选类解谜游戏中存在评估空白。

Method: 设计了一个名为VLATIM的新基准，包含五个渐进阶段，评估从基础视觉接地、领域理解到多步操作和完整解谜的能力，聚焦于逻辑推理与连续动作空间（精确鼠标交互）的结合。

Result: 实验结果显示，大型专有模型虽具备较强的规划能力，但在精确视觉定位上表现不佳，导致整体执行效果远未达到人类水平。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在结合高阶逻辑推理与精细动作执行方面仍存在显著挑战，VLATIM为未来研究提供了衡量和改进该能力的重要工具。

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [103] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于类比推理（AR）的方法，用于缓解大语言模型在开放性问题中生成解决方案时的模式崩溃问题，显著提升了生成结果的多样性与新颖性，并在多个生物医学任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放性科学问题中容易出现模式崩溃，导致生成的解决方案缺乏多样性和新颖性，限制了其在自主科学研究中的应用。

Method: 引入类比推理（AR），通过跨领域问题的类比，基于共享的关系结构生成新类比，并利用这些类比搜索新颖解决方案。

Result: 相比基线方法，AR将解决方案的多样性指标提高了90%-173%，超过50%的生成解为新颖解（基线最低仅1.6%），并在四个生物医学问题中实现显著性能提升：扰动效应预测分布指标提高近13倍，细胞通讯预测AUPRC优于所有基线，脑区交互推断与已有方法Spearman相关系数达0.729，寡核苷酸性质预测在两个数据集上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 类比推理能有效提升大语言模型在开放性问题中生成新颖且多样化解决方案的能力，具有增强现有科学发现方法的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [104] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentRouter，一种通过潜在多模态效用预测实现MLLM路由的方法，能够根据图像-问题查询的多模态需求匹配最合适的大模型，在性能和成本之间实现优化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM路由器仅依赖查询难度估计，难以充分匹配不同模型在OCR、图表理解、空间推理等多模态能力上的异构优势，因此需要更精细的能力适配机制。

Method: LatentRouter将路由建模为反事实多模态效用预测：从图像-问题查询中提取多模态路由胶囊，为每个候选模型分配模型能力令牌，并通过这些状态之间的潜在通信来估计各模型的表现；使用分布化输出头预测反事实质量，并通过有界胶囊校正优化接近决策的准确性。

Result: 在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上，LatentRouter优于固定模型、特征级融合和学习型路由器基线方法；尤其在依赖视觉、布局或推理能力的任务中增益显著，且潜在通信是主要改进来源。

Conclusion: LatentRouter通过共享评分与可用性掩码支持动态模型池切换，实现了高效、灵活的多模态大模型路由，适用于性能导向与性能-成本联合优化场景。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [105] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) 的新方法，用于在低数据场景下更有效地进行数据修剪，通过最大化整体影响并惩罚过度的单个测试贡献，在OpenDataVal基准上表现出色且运行时间具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Shapley值的数据评估方法在少量数据保留时并不适合低价值数据的修剪，因此需要一种更适合小数据情况下的数据选择方法。

Method: 将数据修剪问题建模为一个约束优化问题，目标是最大化总影响同时限制每个测试样本的贡献，从而得到更鲁棒的数据子集。

Result: 在OpenDataVal基准测试中，CDVM在仅保留少量数据的情况下仍展现出强大的性能，并具有较好的计算效率。

Conclusion: CDVM是一种在低资源条件下优于传统Shapley方法的数据修剪策略，能更有效地利用数据归因信息。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于RAG的幻觉检测基准TRIVIA+，解决了现有基准在长上下文和标签噪声方面的不足，并通过实验揭示了当前检测器的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉检测基准（HDBs）缺乏长上下文支持和真实标签噪声，难以有效评估检测器在现实场景中的表现。

Method: 设计并开源了一个新的RAG-based HDB——TRIVIA+，包含最长上下文样本，并提供四种不同类型的噪声标签；通过人类标注确保质量，并在多个SOTA检测器上进行实验评估。

Result: TRIVIA+是目前唯一具备所有理想属性的HDB；实验表明当前检测器仍有提升空间，LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现良好，且标签噪声会显著影响检测性能。

Conclusion: TRIVIA+为幻觉检测研究提供了更贴近现实的评估平台，有望推动RAG任务中检测技术的发展。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [107] [CPEMH: An Agentic Framework for Prompt-Driven Behavior Evaluation and Assurance in Foundation-Model Systems for Mental Health Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11341)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Ivens Portugal,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CPEMH是一个用于评估基于提示的基础模型在心理健康筛查中行为的智能体框架，通过模块化架构实现提示策略的自动设计、评估与选择，提升系统行为的稳定性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了确保基础模型在敏感领域（如心理健康筛查）中的行为可靠性与一致性，需要一种系统化的方法来管理提示驱动的行为变异性。

Method: 提出CPEMH框架，采用模块化智能体架构（包括协调器、推理和评估智能体），自动完成提示策略的设计、评估与选择，并在访谈转录文本的抑郁筛查案例中验证其效果。

Result: 框架在抑郁筛查任务中展现出对模型行为的有效稳定与审计能力，结果表明模块化编排有助于行为保障，且F1分数、偏差和鲁棒性是关键验收标准。

Conclusion: CPEMH为大规模语言系统提供了可追溯、可复现且稳健的行为保证方法，强调在临床敏感场景中应优先考虑行为稳定性而非架构复杂性。

Abstract: This paper presents CPEMH, an agentic framework designed to evaluate prompt-driven behavior in foundation-model systems operating on transcript-based datasets for mental-health screening. CPEMH serves as an engineering methodology for behavioral assurance in large-scale language systems, introducing an orchestrated architecture that autonomously performs the design, evaluation, and selection of prompt strategies, enabling systematic control of behavioral variability across contexts. Its modular agentic design, combining orchestrator, inference, and evaluation agents, ensures traceability, reproducibility, and robustness throughout the prompting lifecycle. A case study on automated depression screening from interview transcripts demonstrates the framework's capacity to stabilize and audit foundation-model behavior in conversational and clinically sensitive domains. Lessons learned emphasize the role of modular orchestration in behavioral assurance, the prioritization of stability over architectural complexity, and the integration of F1, bias, and robustness as core acceptance criteria.

</details>


### [108] [CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11359)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Yanqi Luo,Dishant Beniwal,Songyuan Tang,Hemant Sharma,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVEvolve是一个零代码、自主的LLM驱动框架，用于科学图像数据处理算法的自动发现，通过多轮搜索策略在无需专业编程知识的情况下帮助领域科学家从复杂数据中提取有用算法并促进科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低领域科学家在处理噪声大、标签稀疏或高动态范围的科学图像数据时对专业知识的依赖，解决传统方法需要编程和图像处理技能的障碍。

Method: 提出CVEvolve，采用多轮搜索策略，结合代码执行、评估实现、历史管理、留出测试和可视化检查工具，交替进行算法发现与优化，并利用谱系感知的随机采样平衡探索与开发。

Result: 在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准、布拉格峰检测和高能衍射显微图像分割任务中，CVEvolve发现的算法性能优于基线方法，且通过留出测试识别出泛化能力更强的候选算法。

Conclusion: 零代码、自主的LLM驱动算法开发可有效助力领域科学家将非结构化科学图像数据转化为实用算法，推动下游科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific data processing often requires task-specific algorithms or AI models, creating a barrier for domain scientists who need to analyze their data but may not have extensive computing or image-processing expertise. This barrier is especially pronounced when data are noisy, have a high dynamic range, are sparsely labeled, or are only loosely specified. We introduce CVEvolve, an autonomous agentic harness with a zero-code interface for scientific data-processing algorithm discovery. CVEvolve combines a multi-round search strategy with tools for code execution, evaluation implementation, history management, holdout testing, and optional inspection of scientific data and visual outputs. The search alternates between discovery and improvement actions, and uses lineage-aware stochastic candidate sampling to balance exploration and exploitation. We demonstrate CVEvolve on x-ray fluorescence microscopy image registration, Bragg peak detection, and high-energy diffraction microscopy image segmentation. Across these tasks, CVEvolve discovers algorithms that improve over baseline methods, while holdout test tracking helps identify candidates that generalize better than later over-optimized alternatives. These results show that zero-code, autonomous LLM-powered algorithm development can help domain scientists turn unstructured scientific image data into practical algorithms and downstream scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [109] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于生成式AI的因果公平性分析框架，统一了传统机器学习与生成式模型下的公平性理论，并推导出新的因果分解方法，以量化不同因果路径及模型机制替换对公平性的影响，通过实证验证了方法在检测大语言模型中种族和性别偏见的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能系统在高风险领域的广泛应用，公平性问题日益突出。传统因果公平性研究主要针对标准机器学习设置，而生成式AI能够自主构建多个因果机制，导致现有方法不适用，因此需要新的理论框架来应对这一挑战。

Method: 本文形式化了生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，将其与标准机器学习设置统一于一个共同的理论框架下；推导出新的因果分解结果，以分别量化不同因果路径以及生成模型替代真实世界机制所带来的公平性影响；提出了识别条件和高效估计器来估计相关因果量。

Result: 本文成功建立了适用于生成式AI的因果公平性分析工具，能够在不同数据集上量化大语言模型中由不同因果路径引起的种族和性别偏见，并通过实证分析验证了所提方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为生成式人工智能中的公平性评估提供了 principled 的因果框架，扩展了传统因果公平性的理论边界，有助于更深入理解并缓解生成模型中的社会偏见。

Abstract: Automated systems built on artificial intelligence (AI) are increasingly deployed across high-stakes domains, raising critical concerns about fairness and the perpetuation of demographic disparities that exist in the world. In this context, causal inference provides a principled framework for reasoning about fairness, as it links observed disparities to underlying mechanisms and aligns naturally with human intuition and legal notions of discrimination. Prior work on causal fairness primarily focuses on the standard machine learning setting, where a decision-maker constructs a single predictive mechanism $f_{\widehat Y}$ for an outcome variable $Y$, while inheriting the causal mechanisms of all other covariates from the real world. The generative AI setting, however, is markedly more complex: generative models can sample from arbitrary conditionals over any set of variables, implicitly constructing their own beliefs about all causal mechanisms rather than learning a single predictive function. This fundamental difference requires new developments in causal fairness methodology. We formalize the problem of causal fairness in generative AI and unify it with the standard ML setting under a common theoretical framework. We then derive new causal decomposition results that enable granular quantification of fairness impacts along both (a) different causal pathways and (b) the replacement of real-world mechanisms by the generative model's mechanisms. We establish identification conditions and introduce efficient estimators for causal quantities of interest, and demonstrate the value of our methodology by analyzing race and gender bias in large language models across different datasets.

</details>


### [110] [Causal Algorithmic Recourse: Foundations and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11373)
*Drago Plecko,Collin Wang,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种因果框架来建模算法反事实推理（algorithmic recourse）过程，考虑了现实世界中个体在不同潜在条件下重复决策的情况，并开发了基于copula的算法以及分布无关的方法来推断反事实效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将反事实结果视为固定单元的反事实，忽略了现实世界中的反事实涉及同一人在不同潜在条件下的重复决策问题。

Method: 建立了一个因果框架，对干预前后的结果进行建模，允许潜在变量的部分稳定性和重采样；提出了后反事实稳定性条件，并开发了基于copula的算法来从观测数据中推断反事实效应；在有配对观察数据时，提供了估计copula参数和拟合优度检验的方法；当copula模型被拒绝时，采用分布无关算法直接学习反事实效应。

Result: 在真实和半合成数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性，能够更准确地推断反事实的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为算法反事实提供了更符合现实的建模方式，支持从观测数据中进行有效推断，并在数据满足或不满足模型假设时均有可用算法。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of AI decision-making systems is increasingly important. A key feature of such systems is the ability to provide recommendations for how an individual may reverse a negative decision, a problem known as algorithmic recourse. Existing approaches treat recourse outcomes as counterfactuals of a fixed unit, ignoring that real-world recourse involves repeated decisions on the same individual under possibly different latent conditions. We develop a causal framework that models recourse as a process over pre- and post-intervention outcomes, allowing for partial stability and resampling of latent variables. We introduce post-recourse stability conditions that enable reasoning about recourse from observational data alone, and develop a copula-based algorithm for inferring the effects of recourse under these conditions. For settings where paired observations of the same individual before and after intervention are available (called recourse data), we develop methods for inferring copula parameters and performing goodness-of-fit testing. When the copula model is rejected, we provide a distribution-free algorithm for learning recourse effects directly from recourse data. We demonstrate the value of the proposed methods on real and semi-synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [111] [Revisiting Privacy Preservation in Brain-Computer Interfaces: Conceptual Boundaries, Risk Pathways, and a Protection-Strength Grading Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11386)
*Lei Sun,Xiuqing Mao,Shuai Zhang,Qingyu Zeng,Min Zhao,Jiyuan Li,Wenle Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了脑机接口（BCI）在从实验室走向实际应用过程中面临的隐私保护挑战，提出了一个三维框架来分类现有研究，并强调需在保护数据的同时解耦任务无关的敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着BCI技术向临床和现实世界场景扩展，神经数据及其模型资产可能在生命周期各阶段泄露，带来超出传统数据隐私的风险，亟需系统性隐私保护框架。

Method: 基于通用BCI范式，定义隐私保护边界与对象，分析用户数据隐私与模型隐私的关系，并提出一个包含保护对象、生命周期阶段和主导保护强度水平的三维分类框架。

Result: 将现有工作按保护强度分为四个等级，揭示了当前研究在不同生命周期阶段对不同类型数据的保护不足，特别是在推理和反馈阶段的模型隐私问题。

Conclusion: BCI隐私保护应超越简单的数据隐藏，需结合解耦任务无关敏感信息的方法，在保障隐私的同时维持系统的下游效用，并关注心智隐私与神经伦理风险。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) are moving rapidly from laboratory research into clinical, edge, and real-world settings. Under ISO/IEC 8663:2025, a BCI is a direct communication link between central nervous system activity and external software or hardware systems. This link expands privacy risk beyond raw neural-signal leakage: neural data, derived representations, model assets, and decoded outputs can be re-associated with individuals across collection, transmission, storage, training, inference, and feedback, or used to infer information beyond what a task requires. Starting from the general BCI paradigm, this review deffnes privacy-protection boundaries, protection objects, and the relationship between user data privacy and model privacy within a shared risk pathway. It then proposes a three-dimensional framework - protection object, lifecycle stage, and dominant protection-strength level - to classify existing work into four levels of protection strength. Finally, mental privacy and neuroethical risks are treated as open issues, emphasizing that BCI privacy protection should not only obscure data but also disentangle task-irrelevant sensitive information while preserving downstream utility. Keywords: Brain-computer interface, Neural data privacy, User data privacy, Model privacy, Disentanglement of task-irrelevant sensitive information, Protection-strength grading, Neuroethical risks

</details>


### [112] [Transformer Interpretability from Perspective of Attention and Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11392)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
