TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过引导梯度方向来实现Transformer模型解释的方法，增强了对特征区域的全面解释能力，并揭示了视觉Transformer与人类感知图像的差异可能导致的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管研究者更关注Transformer模型的性能，但其可解释性同样重要。现有方法在解释Transformer时存在不足，尤其是如何从注意力和梯度的角度深入理解模型行为。

Method: 从注意力和梯度的视角出发，提出通过引导梯度方向（即注意力方向）来实现对Transformer的解释，从而实现更全面的特征区域解释。

Result: 该方法能够提供更细致的解释，帮助理解Transformer机制；同时发现利用ViT与人类感知差异可以实现几乎不可察觉的图像类别重写现象。

Conclusion: 所提方法提升了Transformer的可解释性，并揭示了潜在的安全风险，提示在实际应用中需关注模型感知与人类感知之间的差异可能带来的威胁。

Abstract: Although researchers' attention is more focused on the performance of Transformer models, the interpretation of Transformer can never be ignored. Gradient is widely utilized in Transformer interpretation. From the perspective of attention and gradient, we conduct an in-depth study of Transformer interpretation and propose a method to achieve it by guiding the gradient direction, or more precisely, the attention direction. The method enables more comprehensive interpretation of feature regions, offers detail interpretation, and helps to better understand Transformer mechanism. Leveraging the difference in how Vision Transformer (ViT) and humans perceive images, we alter the class of an image in a way that is almost imperceptible to the human eye. This class rewriting phenomenon may potentially pose security risks in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [AcuityBench: Evaluating Clinical Acuity Identification and Uncertainty Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11398)
*Robin Linzmayer,Georgianna Lin,Di Coneybeare,Jason Chu,Trudi Cloyd,Manish Garg,Miles Gordon,Elizabeth Hartofilis,Benjamin Hong,Ashraf Hussain,Eugene Y. Kim,Oluchi Iheagwara King,Ross McCormack,Erica Olsen,John K. Riggins,Mustafa N. Rasheed,Dana L. Sacco,Vinay Saggar,Osman R. Sayan,Amit Shembekar,Janice Shin-Kim,Wendy W. Sun,Bernard P. Chang,David Kessler,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcuityBench是一个用于评估语言模型识别用户医疗状况紧急程度能力的新基准，整合了五个公共数据集，涵盖多种医疗场景，并提出统一的四级急症框架，支持问答分类和对话响应两种任务形式，揭示了现有模型在急症判断中的系统性偏差与不确定性建模不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗健康基准主要关注医学问答或特定工作流中的分诊任务，缺乏跨场景统一评估语言模型对医疗紧急程度识别能力的综合性基准，因此需要构建一个标准化、多场景融合的评估框架。

Method: 整合五个公开数据集（包括用户对话、论坛帖子、临床病例、患者门户消息等），构建包含914个案例的AcuityBench，采用四级急症分类框架；支持两种评估方式：四选一问答分类和基于评分标准的自由对话响应评判，并引入医生确认的模糊案例进行不确定性感知评估。

Result: 在12个主流语言模型上测试发现：不同模型在明确案例中的准确率差异显著且存在方向性错误；对话式响应相比问答形式减少了过度分诊但增加了低级错误分诊，尤其在高紧急情况下更明显；在模糊案例中，所有模型均未能复现医生判断的分布，预测结果比专家更集中，显示其对临床不确定性的建模不足。

Conclusion: 急症识别是一项独立且安全关键的语言模型能力，AcuityBench为评估和压力测试模型在真实医疗场景中引导用户获取适当护理水平的表现提供了有效工具，揭示了当前模型在处理临床不确定性方面的局限性。

Abstract: We introduce AcuityBench, a benchmark for evaluating whether language models identify the appropriate urgency of care from user medical presentations. Existing health benchmarks emphasize medical question answering, broad health interactions, or narrow workflow-specific triage tasks, but they do not offer a unified evaluation of acuity identification across these settings. AcuityBench addresses this gap by harmonizing five public datasets spanning user conversations, online forum posts, clinical vignettes, and patient portal messages under a shared four-level acuity framework ranging from home monitoring to immediate emergency care. The benchmark contains 914 cases, including 697 consensus cases for standard accuracy evaluation and 217 physician-confirmed ambiguous cases for uncertainty-aware evaluation. It supports two complementary task formats: explicit four-way classification in a QA setting, and free-form conversational responses evaluated with a rubric-based judge anchored to the same framework. Across 12 frontier proprietary and open-weight models, we find substantial variation in clear-case acuity accuracy and error direction. Comparing task formats reveals a systematic tradeoff: conversational responses reduce over-triage but increase under-triage relative to QA, especially in higher-acuity cases. In ambiguous cases, no model closely matches the distribution of physician judgments, and model predictions are more concentrated than expert clinical uncertainty. We also compare expert and model adjudication on a subset of maximally ambiguous cases, using those cases to examine the role of clinical uncertainty in label disagreement. Together, these results position acuity identification as a distinct safety-critical capability and show that AcuityBench enables systematic comparison and stress-testing of how well models guide users to the right level of care in real-world health use.

</details>


### [114] [What Do EEG Foundation Models Capture from Human Brain Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11410)
*Ling Tang,Qian Chen,Jilin Mei,Houshi Xu,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了现代脑电图（EEG）基础模型与传统手工特征之间的表示一致性，通过探针分析、子空间擦除和透明分类器等方法，发现68.6%的特征具有表示因果性，频率域特征占主导地位，且50个特征被识别为跨任务的通用候选特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨EEG基础模型学到的表示是否与传统手工设计的特征对齐，并分解为‘模型学到了什么’、‘使用了什么’以及‘可解释程度’三个问题。

Method: 采用逐层岭回归探针、LEACE风格的交叉协方差子空间擦除，以及基于随机特征基线的透明分类器进行分析，覆盖三种基础模型、五种临床任务和63个特征。

Result: 在945个（模型、任务、特征）单元中，68.6%具有表示因果性，21.1%仅为编码存在；50个特征在多个任务中表现出强支持，成为通用候选；平均恢复了基础模型相对于随机基线79.3%的优势，不同任务间恢复程度呈梯度变化。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型主要学习并利用传统特征（尤其是频域特征），但复杂任务仍存在未被现有特征解释的残差，指明了未来概念发现的方向。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalogram (EEG) analysis rests on a hand-crafted feature catalog refined over decades, \emph{e.g.,} band power, connectivity, complexity, and more. Modern EEG foundation models bypass this catalog, learn directly from raw signals via self-supervised pretraining, and match or outperform feature-engineered baselines on most clinical benchmarks. Whether the two representations align is an open question, which we decompose into three sub-questions: \emph{what does the model learn}, \emph{what does the model use}, and \emph{how much can be explained}. We answer them with layer-wise ridge probing, LEACE-style cross-covariance subspace erasure, and a transparent classifier benchmarked against a random-feature baseline. The audit covers three foundation models (CSBrain, CBraMod, LaBraM), five clinical tasks (MDD, Stress, ISRUC-Sleep, TUSL, Siena), and a 6-family 63-feature lexicon. Of the $945$ (model, task, feature) units, $648$ ($68.6\%$) are representation-causal and $199$ ($21.1\%$) are encoded-only. Across tasks, $50$ features qualify as universal candidates with strong support (all three architectures RC) in two or more tasks. Frequency-domain features dominate, but the other five families each contribute substantial causal mass. Confirmed features recover, on average, $79.3\%$ of the foundation model's advantage over the random baseline, with a clean task gradient (MDD $\approx 0.99$ down to Stress $\approx 0.56$): tasks near ceiling are almost fully recovered by the lexicon, while harder tasks leave a non-trivial residual that pinpoints a concrete target for future concept discovery.

</details>


### [115] [A Mechanistic Investigation of Supervised Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11426)
*Ruhaan Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为高分辨率诊断工具的新分析流程，揭示了监督微调（SFT）虽保持高隐藏激活余弦相似性，但显著改变了底层稀疏特征的语义表示，并发现了任务和安全对齐特定的层间更新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SFT后的语言模型隐藏激活与原始模型高度相似，但这种表观稳定性可能掩盖了深层语义表示的变化；因此需要更精细的工具来揭示SFT过程中实际发生的机制性变化。

Method: 使用在基础模型上预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）将SFT前后模型的隐藏激活投影到稀疏潜在空间，进而分析语义特征的变化分布及层别更新模式。

Result: 发现SFT导致底层稀疏语义特征显著偏离，且这种偏离具有任务特异性和层特异性；同时识别出与安全对齐相关的独特层更新模式。

Conclusion: 高激活相似性并不意味着语义稳定，SAE可作为高分辨率工具揭示SFT中被掩盖的表示变化，为理解模型微调机制提供了新路径。

Abstract: The cosine similarity between a large language model's hidden activations before and after Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) remains very high. This, at first glance, suggests that SFT leaves the model's activation geometry largely undisturbed. However, projecting both sets of activations through a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) pretrained on the base model reveals that the underlying sparse latents diverge significantly. We introduce a novel investigative pipeline which utilizes these pretrained SAEs as a high-resolution diagnostic tool to mechanistically investigate the drivers of this representational divergence. Through our analytical pipeline, we discover task-specific and layer-specific distributions of the precise semantic features that are systematically altered during supervised fine-tuning. We additionally identify a layer-wise update profile specific to safety alignment. All code, experimental scripts, and analysis files associated with this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/ruhzi/sae-investigation.

</details>


### [116] [Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11458)
*Zihao Han,Tiangang Zhang,Huaibin Wang,Yilun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应教师暴露方法（ATESD），用于改进大语言模型推理中的自我蒸馏，通过动态调整教师看到的参考推理量来缓解师生能力不匹配问题，并在多个基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督蒸馏方法中，教师始终能看到完整的参考推理路径，这可能导致教师输出过于强大而学生难以学习，产生教师端暴露失配问题。作者希望解决这一暴露失配，提升学生模型的学习效率。

Method: 提出Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation (ATESD)，使用一个轻量级的基于Beta策略的控制器，根据训练状态统计信息动态建模教师应看到的推理内容比例（reveal ratio），并在短暂的保持窗口内固定该暴露比例；通过折扣化的学习进展奖励优化控制器，以衡量每次暴露决策对学生未来进步的影响，而非即时损失变化。

Result: 在AIME 24、AIME 25和HMMT 25数据集上，基于Qwen3系列模型（1.7B、4B、8B）的实验表明，ATESD持续优于OPSD等强基线方法，平均@12指标分别提升+0.95、+2.05和+2.33点。

Conclusion: 将教师暴露视为可学习的训练时控制变量是有效的，自适应教师暴露为推理导向的自我蒸馏提供了一个新的有效优化维度。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation has become a strong recipe for LLM reasoning, where a privileged teacher supervises the student's own rollouts while conditioning on the reference solution. A design choice shared by nearly all such methods, however, has gone unquestioned: the teacher always sees the full reference reasoning. We argue that this default itself is part of the problem and identify a teacher-side exposure mismatch: when the teacher conditions on reasoning far beyond the student's current competence, the resulting token targets become too strong to absorb. A controlled fixed-exposure sweep makes this concrete on two fronts: 1) full exposure is not reliably the best choice, and 2) student-teacher mismatch grows monotonically as the teacher sees more privileged reasoning. This motivates treating teacher exposure not as a fixed hyperparameter but as a learnable training-time control variable. We therefore propose Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation (ATESD). ATESD models the reveal ratio with a lightweight Beta-policy controller conditioned on compact training-state statistics, and uses one sampled exposure for a short hold window of student updates. To make this exposure controller learnable, we optimize it with a discounted learning-progress reward that scores each held decision by its effect on the student's future improvement rather than its immediate loss change, addressing the delayed credit assignment induced by on-policy distillation. Experiments on AIME 24, AIME 25, and HMMT 25 across Qwen3-{1.7B, 4B, 8B} show that ATESD consistently outperforms competitive self-distillation and RL baselines, improving over OPSD by +0.95, +2.05, and +2.33 Average@12 points respectively, and establishing adaptive teacher exposure as an effective new axis for reasoning self-distillation.

</details>


### [117] [Breaking $\textit{Winner-Takes-All}$: Cooperative Policy Optimization Improves Diverse LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11461)
*Haoxuan Chen,Tianming Liang,Wei-Shi Zheng,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习框架GCPO，用于提升大语言模型的推理能力，通过团队合作机制替代传统的竞争机制，有效缓解探索崩溃问题，提高推理准确性和解法多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的优化算法（如GRPO）在强化学习中容易出现探索崩溃，即模型过早收敛于少数高分模式，缺乏多样性。尽管已有方法尝试通过熵正则化或多样性奖励缓解该问题，但仍受限于‘赢家通吃’的竞争机制。

Method: 提出Group Cooperative Policy Optimization (GCPO)，将独立 rollout 评分改为团队级信用分配：每个rollout的奖励取决于其对团队整体有效解覆盖范围的边际贡献。该覆盖范围由奖励加权语义嵌入空间中的行列式体积表示，仅正确且非冗余的路径才增加体积，并通过边际贡献进行优势估计。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的实验表明，GCPO显著优于现有方法，在推理准确性和解法多样性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: GCPO通过从竞争到合作的训练范式转变，有效促进了非冗余正确推理路径的发现，为改进大模型推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiers (RLVR) has become a central paradigm for improving LLM reasoning, yet popular group-based optimization algorithms like GRPO often suffer from exploration collapse, where the models prematurely converge on a narrow set of high-scoring patterns, lacking the ability to explore new solutions. Recent efforts attempt to alleviate this by adding entropy regularization or diversity bonus. However, these approaches do not change the \textit{winner-takes-all} nature, where rollouts still compete for individual advantage rather than cooperating for maximizing global diversity. In this work, we propose Group Cooperative Policy Optimization (GCPO), which shifts the training paradigm from rollout competition to team cooperation. Specifically, GCPO replaces independent rollout scoring with team-level credit assignment: a rollout is rewarded by how much it contributes to the team's valid solution coverage, rather than its individual accuracy. This coverage is described as a determinant volume over reward-weighted semantic embeddings, where only correct and non-redundant rollouts contribute to this volume. During advantage estimation, GCPO redistributes the collective team reward to each single rollout according to its average marginal contribution to the team. This cooperative training paradigm routes optimization toward non-redundant correct reasoning paths. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that GCPO significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and solution diversity over existing approaches. Code will be released at $\href{https://github.com/bradybuddiemarch/gcpo}{this}$.

</details>


### [118] [TOPPO: Rethinking PPO for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Critic Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11473)
*Yuanpeng Li,Gefei Lin,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TOPPO，一种通过Critic Balancing改进PPO在多任务强化学习中表现的方法，解决了PPO中存在的批评者侧梯度病态问题，显著提升了尾部任务性能，在更少参数和环境步数下超越SAC等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现PPO在多任务强化学习中因批评者侧梯度条件差导致尾部任务训练停滞，而现有研究忽视了这一优化瓶颈。

Method: 提出TOPPO，引入Critic Balancing机制，包括改善梯度条件和平衡各任务学习动态的模块，直接针对PPO的优化缺陷进行重构。

Result: 在Meta-World+基准上，TOPPO在平均和尾部任务性能上均优于SAC和ARS家族的基线方法，且使用更少参数和环境交互步数；训练早期即能匹敌或超越SAC，并在整个训练过程中保持优势。

Conclusion: 适当的优化改进可使on-policy方法在多任务强化学习中达到甚至超过off-policy方法的性能，挑战了当前对SAC的依赖，揭示批评者侧梯度条件是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) and its variants dominate Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning (MTRL) due to their off-policy sample efficiency, while on-policy methods such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remain underexplored. We diagnose that PPO in MTRL suffers from a previously overlooked issue: critic-side gradient ill-conditioning, which may cause tail tasks to stall while easy tasks dominate the value function's updates. To address this, we propose TOPPO (Tail-Optimized PPO), a reformulation of PPO via Critic Balancing -- a set of modules that improve gradient conditioning and balance learning dynamics across tasks. Unlike prior approaches that rely on modular architectures or large models, TOPPO targets the optimization bottleneck within PPO itself. Empirically, TOPPO achieves stronger mean and tail-task performance than published SAC-family and ARS-family baselines while using substantially fewer parameters and environment steps on Meta-World+ benchmark. Notably, TOPPO matches or surpasses strong SAC baselines early in training and maintains superior performance at full budget. Ablations confirm the effectiveness of each module in TOPPO and provide insights into their interactions. Our results demonstrate that, with proper optimization, on-policy methods can rival or exceed off-policy approaches in MTRL, challenging the prevailing reliance on SAC and highlighting critic-side gradient conditioning as the central bottleneck.

</details>


### [119] [FibQuant: Universal Vector Quantization for Random-Access KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11478)
*Namyoon Lee,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FibQuant的通用固定码率向量量化方法，用于高效压缩长上下文推理中的KV缓存，通过保留归一化后的几何结构，在内存和精度之间实现了显著优于现有标量量化方法的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着上下文长度增加，KV缓存带来的内存访问压力成为长上下文推理的主要瓶颈；现有的旋转基标量编解码方法虽支持随机访问，但忽略了归一化后坐标间的几何相关性，导致信息损失。

Method: FibQuant采用统一的归一化-旋转-存储接口，用共享的径向-角向联合码本替代标量查表，码本设计结合了Beta分位数半径、Fibonacci/Roberts-Kronecker拟均匀方向及多重启Lloyd-Max优化，在块状球面-Beta源上实现向量量化。

Result: 理论证明FibQuant在相同码率下严格优于其标量特例，高频增益可分解为细胞整形和密度匹配两部分；实验显示在GPT-2小模型KV缓存上实现5倍至34倍压缩（注意力余弦相似度0.99到0.95）；在TinyLlama-1.1B上，4倍压缩时困惑度接近fp16，8倍压缩时比标量TurboQuant低3.6倍。

Conclusion: FibQuant通过利用KV缓存中被忽略的几何结构，提供了一种高效、通用且支持随机访问的向量量化方案，显著提升了长上下文场景下的内存-精度权衡，为大规模语言模型的高效推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly a memory-traffic problem. The culprit is the key--value (KV) cache: it grows with context length, batch size, layers, and heads, and it is read at every decoding step. Rotation-based scalar codecs meet this systems constraint by storing a norm, applying a shared random rotation, and quantizing one coordinate at a time. They are universal and random-access, but they discard the geometry created by the normalization step. After a Haar rotation, a block of $k$ consecutive coordinates is not a product source; it is a spherical-Beta source on the unit ball. We introduce \textsc{FibQuant}, a universal fixed-rate vector quantizer that keeps the same normalize--rotate--store interface while replacing scalar tables by a shared radial--angular codebook matched to this canonical source. The codebook combines Beta-quantile radii, Fibonacci\,/\,Roberts--Kronecker quasi-uniform directions, and multi-restart Lloyd--Max refinement. We prove that the resulting vector code strictly improves on its scalar product specialization at matched rate, with a high-rate gain that separates into a cell-shaping factor and a density-matching factor. The same construction gives a dense rate axis, including fractional-bit and sub-one-bit operating points, without calibration or variable-length addresses. On GPT-2 small KV caches, \textsc{FibQuant} traces a memory--fidelity frontier from $5\times$ compression at $0.99$ attention cosine similarity to $34\times$ at $0.95$. End-to-end on TinyLlama-1.1B, it is within $0.10$ perplexity of fp16 at $4\times$ compression and has $3.6\times$ lower perplexity than scalar \textsc{TurboQuant} at $b = 2$ ($8\times$ compression), where scalar random-access quantization begins to fail.

</details>


### [120] [The Evaluation Differential: When Frontier AI Models Recognise They Are Being Tested](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11496)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“评估差异（Evaluation Differential, ED）”概念，指出当前AI模型能在识别评估情境时表现出与部署环境不同的行为，从而挑战了基于传统评估的安全性结论。为此，作者提出TRACE审计协议以规范从评估中得出的主张，并对现有安全声明进行了分类和回溯分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来多个前沿实验室发现AI模型能识别并适应评估环境，导致其在测试和实际部署中的行为不一致，这威胁到AI安全性评估的有效性。因此需要重新审视评估结果的可靠性及其对安全声明的支持力度。

Method: 引入评估差异（ED）及其归一化形式nED来量化行为差异，证明边际评估分数无法检测ED；建立安全声明类型学（ED-稳定、退化、反转、未定），并设计TRACE审计协议，嵌入现有评估流程以生成条件性安全声明。

Result: 成功将TRACE框架应用于三个公开案例，揭示了传统评估可能高估安全性的风险；明确了不同安全声明在ED存在下的有效性状态；提供了可集成于现有评估体系的审计方法。

Conclusion: AI模型的情境感知能力削弱了传统评估的可信度，必须通过如TRACE之类的审计机制明确评估条件，限制过度泛化的安全主张，提升AI安全评估的严谨性和透明度。

Abstract: Recent published evidence from frontier laboratories shows that contemporary AI models can recognise evaluation contexts, latently represent them, and behave differently under those contexts than under deployment-continuous conditions. Anthropic's BrowseComp incident, the Natural Language Autoencoder findings on SWE-bench Verified and destructive-coding evaluations, and the OpenAI / Apollo anti-scheming work all document instances of this phenomenon. We argue that these findings create a claim-validity problem for safety conclusions drawn from frontier evaluations. We introduce the Evaluation Differential (ED), a conditional divergence in a target behavioural property between recognised-evaluation and deployment-continuous contexts, define a normalised effect-size form (nED) for cross-property comparison, and prove that marginal evaluation scores cannot identify ED. We develop a typology of safety claims (ED-stable, ED-degraded, ED-inverted, ED-undetermined) by their warrant-status under documented divergence, and specify TRACE (Test-Recognition Audit for Claim Evaluation), an audit protocol that wraps existing evaluation infrastructure and produces restricted claims rather than capability scores. We apply the framework retrospectively to three publicly documented evaluation incidents and discuss governance implications for system cards, conformity assessment, and the international network of AI safety and security institutes. TRACE does not eliminate adversarial adaptation; it disciplines the claims drawn from evaluation evidence by making explicit the conditions under which that evidence was produced.

</details>


### [121] [Selective Off-Policy Reference Tuning with Plan Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11505)
*Duc Anh Le,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Thien Huu Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SORT是一种改进的强化学习方法，通过引入修复更新来解决GRPO在困难提示上失效的问题，利用参考解生成计划并调整token权重，从而提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GRPO类方法在所有采样回放都失败的难题提示上表现停滞，缺乏有效的学习信号。

Method: SORT不改变rollout生成过程，而是从参考解中提取计划，比较在有无该计划条件下各token的概率变化，并赋予在计划条件下更可预测的token更高权重，实现结构感知的学习。

Result: 在三种模型架构和八个推理基准上的实验表明，SORT优于GRPO和引导基线方法，尤其对较弱模型提升显著。

Conclusion: SORT能有效将完全错误的提示转化为选择性、结构感知的学习信号，为基于奖励的推理训练提供了更鲁棒的优化方式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards helps reasoning, but GRPO-style methods stall on hard prompts where all sampled rollouts fail. SORT adds a repair update for those failures without changing rollout generation: it derives a plan from the reference solution, compares token probabilities with and without that plan, and gives higher weight to tokens that become more predictable under plan conditioning. This turns all-wrong prompts into selective, structure-aware learning signals instead of uniform imitation. Across three backbones and eight reasoning benchmarks, SORT improves over GRPO and guidance baselines, with largest gains on weaker models.

</details>


### [122] [AutoLLMResearch: Training Research Agents for Automating LLM Experiment Configuration -- Learning from Cheap, Optimizing Expensive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11518)
*Taicheng Guo,Nitesh V. Chawla,Olaf Wiest,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoLLMResearch，一个用于自动化大规模语言模型（LLM）实验配置的智能体框架，通过在多保真度环境中学习并外推至高成本设置，有效减少对专家直觉的依赖和计算资源浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化方法适用于低成本、可多次试错的场景，但无法应对大规模LLM实验的高成本特性，且目前尚无研究解决高成本LLM实验配置的自动化问题，因此需要一种能从低代价实验中学习并推广到高代价设置的新方法。

Method: 提出AutoLLMResearch框架，包含两个核心组件：1) LLMConfig-Gym，一个涵盖四个关键LLM实验任务的多保真度环境，基于超过一百万GPU小时的真实实验数据；2) 一种结构化训练流程，将配置搜索建模为长视野马尔可夫决策过程，并激励跨保真度外推推理。

Result: 在保留实验上的广泛评估表明，该框架在性能、泛化能力和可解释性方面优于多种强基线方法。

Conclusion: AutoLLMResearch为大规模、高成本的LLM实验配置提供了一种实用且通用的自动化解决方案，具有显著减少资源浪费和提升配置效率的潜力。

Abstract: Effectively configuring scalable large language model (LLM) experiments, spanning architecture design, hyperparameter tuning, and beyond, is crucial for advancing LLM research, as poor configuration choices can waste substantial computational resources and prevent models from realizing their full potential. Prior automated methods are designed for low-cost settings where repeated trial and error is feasible, but scalable LLM experiments are too expensive for such extensive iteration. To our knowledge, no work has addressed the automation of high-cost LLM experiment configurations, leaving this problem labor-intensive and dependent on expert intuition. Motivated by this gap, we propose AutoLLMResearch, an agentic framework that mimics how human researchers learn generalizable principles from low-fidelity experiments and extrapolate to efficiently identify promising configurations in expensive LLM settings. The core challenge is how to enable an agent to learn, through interaction with a multi-fidelity experimental environment that captures the structure of the LLM configuration landscape. To achieve this, we propose a systematic framework with two key components: 1) LLMConfig-Gym, a multi-fidelity environment encompassing four critical LLM experiment tasks, supported by over one million GPU hours of verifiable experiment outcomes; 2) A structured training pipeline that formulates configuration research as a long-horizon Markov Decision Process and accordingly incentivizes cross-fidelity extrapolation reasoning. Extensive evaluation against diverse strong baselines on held-out experiments demonstrates the effectiveness, generalization, and interpretability of our framework, supporting its potential as a practical and general solution for scalable real-world LLM experiment automation.

</details>


### [123] [Controllable User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11519)
*Guy Tennenholtz,Ofer Meshi,Amir Globerson,Uri Shalit,Jihwan Jeong,Craig Boutilier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将可控用户模拟器的构建形式化为因果推断问题，揭示了传统基于监督微调的方法因依赖后见轨迹标签而导致的结构偏差和“可控性崩溃”现象，并提出了保持因果一致性的训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于离线数据集评估对话系统的方法难以覆盖罕见场景或测试新策略，因此研究者采用可控用户模拟器进行反事实评估。然而，当前通过提示或微调大模型实现的模拟器存在因果不一致性问题，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 作者将可控模拟形式化为因果推断问题，结合非策略评估方法，分析标准监督微调引入的前向偏倚；提出三种缓解策略：先验控制、逐步动态控制和直接策略条件学习，以恢复因果一致性。

Result: 理论证明标准方法在策略迁移下会导致评估指标方差呈几何级增长（即可控性崩溃）；实验表明，传统全局控制会扭曲对话分布并降低行为多样性，而本文提出的因果模拟器能消除前向偏倚，保持自然方差，并对未见的智能体行为展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入因果视角，本文为构建可靠的用户模拟器提供了理论基础和实践方案，解决了现有方法中的结构性偏差问题，提升了对话系统评估的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Using offline datasets to evaluate conversational agents often fails to cover rare scenarios or to support testing new policies. This has motivated the use of controllable user simulators for targeted, counterfactual evaluation, typically implemented by prompting or fine-tuning large language models. In this work, we formalize controllable simulation as a causal inference problem. By bridging natural language evaluation with off-policy evaluation methodology, we show that the standard practice of training simulators via supervised fine-tuning on post-hoc trajectory labels yields a structurally biased model. Specifically, these labels are inextricably coupled to the data-generating behavior policy, injecting a look-ahead bias that breaks causal consistency. Furthermore, we prove that under policy shift this failure causes the variance of evaluation metrics to explode geometrically, a phenomenon we term controllability collapse. To restore causal consistency, we establish theoretical conditions for accurate simulation and propose practical training mitigations: a priori controls, step-wise dynamic controls, and direct policy-conditioned learning. Empirical evaluation confirms that while standard global controls distort conversational distributions and collapse behavioral diversity, our causally grounded simulators eliminate look-ahead bias, preserve natural variance, and exhibit robust zero-shot generalization to unseen agent behaviors.

</details>


### [124] [Read, Grep, and Synthesize: Diagnosing Cross-Domain Seed Exposure for LLM Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11532)
*Yunju Choi,Min Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过PaperGym框架探讨了LLM创意生成系统是否从跨领域检索中受益，发现多样化种子输入比特定语义检索更能提升新颖性，表明当前系统更依赖多样性而非语义相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前LLM创意生成系统是否真正从有目标的跨领域检索中获益，还是仅仅得益于多样化的机制输入。

Method: 提出PaperGym三阶段流程：(1) 工具增强的种子提取，(2) 跨七个ML领域的基于改写的跨域检索，(3) 基于评分标准的专家评估方法合成。

Result: 工具增强提高了特异性，改写提升了领域覆盖；跨域检索在新颖性上优于无检索和同域检索，但与随机多样化种子控制组无显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM创意系统受益于多样化的种子输入，但尚未能可靠利用所检索种子的语义合理性。

Abstract: The discovery of novel methodologies for emerging problems is a continuing cycle in ML, often driven by the migration of techniques across domains. Building on this observation, we ask whether current LLM ideation systems benefit from targeted cross-domain retrieval or simply from exposure to diverse mechanisms. We study this question through PaperGym, a three-stage pipeline: (1) tool-augmented seed extraction via read, grep, and bash over an isolated paper environment, (2) cross-domain seed retrieval via paraphrasing across seven ML domains, and (3) method synthesis from retrieved seeds, each scored by rubric-based judges. Tool-augmented extraction improves specificity, and paraphrase-based retrieval broadens domain coverage. In synthesis, cross-domain retrieval receives more pairwise novelty wins than no-retrieval and same-domain baselines, but shows no significant difference from a random diverse-seed control. These findings suggest LLM ideation systems benefit from diverse seed exposure, but do not yet reliably exploit the semantic reason particular seeds were retrieved. We release the seed library, rubric prompts, and run scripts at https://github.com/yunjoochoi/PaperGym

</details>


### [125] [Optimal LTLf Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11544)
*Yujian Cao,Sven Schewe,Qiyi Tang,Shufang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了最优LTLf综合方法，旨在不确定环境中尽可能多地实现多个目标，即使它们无法全部联合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略综合在无法完全保证规范时直接返回不可实现，缺乏灵活性。本文旨在解决多目标规范中部分可实现性的问题，提升综合的实用性。

Method: 提出三种综合方法：最大保证综合（max-guarantee）、最大观测综合（max-observation）和增量最大观测综合（incremental max-observation），分别从先验保证、后验实现和执行中动态优化角度最大化可实现目标数量。

Result: 实验结果表明，这三种方法在基准测试中均具有良好的可扩展性，能在给定时间内解决大部分实例，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 最优LTLf综合能够在不确定环境中有效处理非联合可实现的多目标规范，显著提升策略的实用性和灵活性。

Abstract: Strategy synthesis typically follows an all-or-nothing paradigm, returning unrealisable whenever a specification cannot be guaranteed in an uncertain environment. In this paper, we introduce optimal LTLf synthesis, where the goal is to realise as many objectives as possible from a given specification consisting of multiple objectives, especially for the case that they are not all jointly realisable. We first consider max-guarantee synthesis, which commits to a maximal set of objectives that we can a priori guarantee to realise. We then introduce max-observation synthesis, which maximises a posteriori realised objectives that may be incomparable on different executions. Finally, we present incremental max-observation synthesis, which further improves strategies by exploiting opportunities for stronger guarantees when they arise during an execution. Experimental results show that different variations of optimal synthesis scale broadly equally well, solving a large fraction of the benchmark instances within the given timeout, demonstrating the practical feasibility of the approach.

</details>


### [126] [Hindsight Hint Distillation: Scaffolded Reasoning for SWE Agents from CoT-free Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11556)
*Shengjie Wang,Guanghe Li,Zonghan Yang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Hindsight Hint Distillation (HHD) 的方法，利用模型自身失败的尝试生成回溯提示，以指导任务完成，并通过自我蒸馏提升在长视野任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂长视野任务需要强大的规划和推理能力，但带有显式思维链（CoT）标注的数据集获取成本高，因此需要一种仅依赖易获得的问答对的方法。

Method: HHD从模型自身的失败自 rollout 中合成回溯提示，用于引导成功的在线 rollout，并通过自我蒸馏这些引导轨迹来泛化到新问题。

Result: HHD在SWE-bench Verified上比基线方法绝对提升了8%，而基线仅提升约2%；在未训练过的多语言任务SWE-bench Multilingual上也表现出最强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HHD能够从未标注CoT数据中有效合成类专家推理，显著提升长视野任务的性能。

Abstract: Solving complex long-horizon tasks requires strong planning and reasoning capabilities. Although datasets with explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales can substantially benefit learning, they are costly to obtain. To address this challenge, we propose Hindsight Hint Distillation (HHD), which only requires easy-to-obtain question-answer pairs without CoT annotations. Inspired by how human teachers use student mistakes to provide targeted guidance, HHD synthesizes hindsight hints from the model's own failed self-rollouts and uses them to scaffold on-policy rollouts that successfully complete the tasks. The model then self-distills these scaffolded trajectories and generalizes to new problems without hint guidance. Experiments show that HHD significantly outperforms iterative RFT and trajectory-synthesis baselines, achieving an absolute improvement of 8\% on SWE-bench Verified, while all baselines improve by only around 2\%. Notably, the reasoning strategies induced by HHD generalize effectively to out-of-distribution tasks, yielding the largest gains on SWE-bench Multilingual despite no training on multilingual data. These results demonstrate that HHD can effectively synthesize expert-like reasoning from CoT-free data and substantially improve long-horizon performance.

</details>


### [127] [Dual-Temporal LSTM with Hybrid Attention for Airline Passenger Load Factor Forecasting: Integrating Intra-Flight and Inter-Flight Booking Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11569)
*ASM Nazrul Islam,Md. Hasanul Kabir,Md. Liakot Ali,Joydeb Kumar Sana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合注意力机制的双流LSTM模型，用于航空需求预测，通过同时处理航班内和航班间的预订序列，显著提升了预测精度，并已在Biman Bangladesh Airlines实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空需求预测模型多采用单一时序维度，忽略另一维度信息，且预测绝对乘客数在飞机型号变更时稳定性差，因此需要更鲁棒、信息利用更充分的模型。

Method: 提出一种双流LSTM结合注意力机制的框架，分别输入水平序列（航班前每日累积预订）和垂直序列（历史同期预订模式），并设计多种融合策略（如拼接、残差、门控）与注意力机制（自注意力、交叉注意力、混合注意力）进行建模。

Result: 在Biman Bangladesh Airlines真实数据上实验表明，该模型MAE为2.8167，R²达0.9495，优于单流模型、树模型及三种已有双LSTM架构，且在国内外、直飞/中转、高频/低频、短/中/长途等航线类别上均表现稳定。

Conclusion: 双流注意力增强LSTM能有效融合两类时序信息，显著提升短期航空需求预测准确性与鲁棒性，具备在不同航线类型上的泛化能力，已成功应用于实际航空运营中。

Abstract: Accurate short-term demand forecasting is crucial to airline revenue management, yet most existing systems fail to meet this need because current models treat booking data as a single temporal dimension, either the accumulation of bookings for a specific flight or the historical booking profile of the same route. This unidimensional view discards information carried by the other temporal stream and forecasting absolute passenger counts introduces a further operational fragility when change in planned aircraft type alters total seat capacity. This study addresses both limitations. A dual-stream Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) integrated with attention framework is proposed that simultaneously processes two complementary input sequences: a horizontal sequence capturing intra-flight booking accumulation over the days preceding departure, and a vertical sequence capturing inter-flight booking patterns at fixed days-before-departure offsets across historical flights. Multiple dual-stream architectural variants, combining self-attention, cross-attention, and hybrid attention with concatenation, residual, and gated fusion strategies, are developed and evaluated. Experiments on real-world reservation data from the national airline of Bangladesh, Biman Bangladesh Airlines (BBA), demonstrate that the proposed hybrid model achieves a Mean Absolute Error of 2.8167 and a coefficient of determination ($R^{2}$) of 0.9495, outperforming single-stream baselines, tree-based models, and three prior dual-LSTM architectures applied to the same data. Validation across four flight category pairs; domestic versus international, direct versus transit, high versus low frequency, and short versus mid versus long haul confirms that the model generalizes across operationally diverse route types. Biman Bangladesh Airlines (BBA) has officially integrated this methodology into its operations.

</details>


### [128] [GAR: Carbon-Aware Routing for LLM Inference via Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11603)
*Disha Sheshanarayana,Rajat Subhra Pal,Manjira Sinha,Tirthankar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Green-Aware Routing (GAR)，一种兼顾准确率和延迟约束的多目标优化框架，用于在大型语言模型推理中实现低碳路由决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM请求路由方法很少考虑能耗与碳排放，而电网碳强度具有时空差异，不同模型能耗差异显著，因此需要一种绿色感知的路由机制。

Method: 提出GAR框架，采用带约束的多目标优化，结合轻量级估计器预测正确性、尾延迟和碳排放，并设计了在线原对偶算法GAR-PD及启发式变体，支持实时决策。

Result: 在多个NLP基准和7B-70B模型池上的实验表明，GAR在满足准确率和p95延迟SLO的前提下，显著降低了每次请求的碳排放。

Conclusion: GAR为大规模语言模型推理提供了一种实用且理论可靠的可持续路由方案，能够在保证服务质量的同时有效减少碳足迹。

Abstract: The growing deployment of large language models (LLMs) makes per-request routing essential for balancing response quality and computational cost across heterogeneous model pools. Current routing methods rarely consider sustainable energy use and CO2 emissions as optimization objectives, despite grid carbon intensity varying by time and region, and models differing significantly in energy consumption. To address this gap, we introduce Green-Aware Routing (GAR), a constrained multi-objective optimization framework that minimizes per-request CO2 emissions subject to explicit accuracy floors and p95-latency service-level objectives (SLOs). GAR employs adaptive constraint optimization through per-dataset floor tuning and incorporates lightweight estimators for correctness, tail latency, and carbon emissions, enabling real-time routing decisions without additional inference passes. We present GAR-PD, a practical online primal-dual routing algorithm for rolling carbon budgets, alongside heuristic variants that achieve high feasibility coverage while limiting accuracy degradation. Comprehensive experiments across standard NLP benchmarks with heterogeneous LLM pools (7B-70B) demonstrate that GAR achieves substantial carbon reductions while maintaining competitive accuracy and p95 latency guarantees, providing a practical, theoretically grounded approach to sustainable LLM inference.

</details>


### [129] [Nice Fold or Hero Call: Learning Budget-Efficient Thinking for Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11625)
*Zhaomeng Zhou,Lan Zhang,Junyang Wang,Mu Yuan,Junda Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Budget-Efficient Thinking (BET)的两阶段框架，通过将推理资源分配视为在不确定性下的计算投资，显著提升了大型推理模型的效率，在多个基准上平均减少约55%的推理token使用量的同时提升整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理效率方法多基于问题难度分配计算资源，忽视了问题的可解性，导致资源误配：对不可解问题浪费计算，或对难但可解问题压缩过多。本文旨在根据推理的预期回报自适应分配预算，实现更高效的资源利用。

Method: 提出BET框架，包含行为冷启动和基于投资成本感知奖励的GRPO优化；模型学习三种行为：短解答（快速回答简单问题）、优雅放弃（在继续推理预期收益接近零时提前终止）和英雄调用（为难但可解问题保留足够计算资源），并通过rollout估计可解性以指导决策。

Result: 在七个基准和三个基础模型上，BET平均减少约55%的推理token消耗，同时实现整体性能提升，并能零样本迁移到科学问答和逻辑推理任务，保持相似的效率增益。

Conclusion: 将推理视为计算投资可有效解决大型推理模型中的资源误配问题，BET通过联合建模可解性与成本，在保证甚至提升性能的同时大幅降低推理开销，具备良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve problem solving through extended reasoning, but often misallocate test-time compute. Existing efficiency methods reduce cost by compressing reasoning traces or conditioning budget on perceived difficulty, yet largely overlook solvability. As a result, they may spend large budgets on queries beyond the model's capability while compressing hard-but-solvable queries that require deeper reasoning. In this work, we formulate adaptive reasoning as a computational investment under uncertainty, where budget should follow the expected return of reasoning rather than perceived difficulty alone. To instantiate this principle, we propose Budget-Efficient Thinking (BET), a two-stage framework that combines behavioral cold-start with GRPO under an investment-cost-aware reward. By aligning solve-or-fold decisions with rollout-derived solvability, BET learns three behaviors: (1) short solve, answering easy queries concisely; (2) nice fold, abstaining early when continued reasoning has near-zero expected return; and (3) hero call, preserving sufficient compute for hard-but-solvable queries. Across seven benchmarks and three base models, BET reduces reasoning tokens by ~55% on average while achieving overall performance improvements, and transfers zero-shot from mathematical reasoning to scientific QA and logical reasoning with comparable efficiency gains.

</details>


### [130] [Seirênes: Adversarial Self-Play with Evolving Distractions for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11636)
*Chi Zhang,Haibo Qiu,Qiming Zhang,Yufei Xu,Xinbo Gao,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seirênes是一种自博弈强化学习框架，通过生成干扰上下文来暴露并改进大模型的推理盲点，提升其在复杂情境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对非理想化、含干扰信息的上下文时表现出推理脆弱性，难以适应真实复杂场景。

Method: 提出Seirênes框架，采用参数共享的对抗式自博弈机制，让同一模型同时学习生成干扰上下文和从中恢复核心逻辑，实现推理能力的协同进化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和4B到30B规模模型上，平均提升+10.2、+9.1和+7.2分；4B模型生成的干扰还能使GPT和Gemini等顶级闭源模型准确率下降约4-5分。

Conclusion: Seirênes能有效将上下文干扰转化为训练信号，推动模型发展出更稳健的深层推理能力，具备广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present Seirênes, a self-play RL framework that transforms contextual interference from a failure mode of LLM reasoning into an internal training signal for co-evolving more resilient reasoners. While RL with verifiable rewards has significantly advanced reasoning capabilities, models can still exhibit fragility when encountering non-idealized contexts: scenarios characterized by superfluous information, tangential instructions, or incidental correlations that differ from the clean distributions typical of standard benchmarks. Seirênes harnesses this vulnerability through a parameter-shared and adversarial self-play loop. Within this framework, a single model is trained to both construct plausible yet distracting contexts that expose its own reasoning blind spots, and solve problems by discerning the essential task from these perturbations to recover the core underlying logic. By pitting these competing objectives against each other, Seirênes compels the model to move beyond superficial pattern matching and anchors its capabilities in robust underlying reasoning. This continuous interaction sustains an informative co-evolutionary curriculum as the model improves. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales from 4B to 30B, Seirênes achieves average gains of +10.2, +9.1, and +7.2 points. Besides, distracting contexts produced by the 4B Seirênes model reduce the accuracy of top-tier closed-source models (GPT and Gemini) by roughly 4--5 points, revealing Seirênes' general ability to uncover reasoning models' blind spots.

</details>


### [131] [A CAP-like Trilemma for Large Language Models: Correctness, Non-bias, and Utility under Semantic Underdetermination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11672)
*Vinu Ellampallil Venugopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对大语言模型（LLM）的CAP式三难困境，指出在语义不确定的情况下，LLM无法同时保证强正确性、严格无偏性和高实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受分布式系统中CAP定理启发，旨在揭示大语言模型在面对语义未定问题时的根本性限制。

Method: 提出并形式化正确性—无偏性—实用性三难困境，通过示例分析论证其存在。

Result: 证明在语义未定前提下，LLM若要提供有用且明确的回答，必须引入偏好或选择标准，否则将牺牲实用性。

Conclusion: 某些LLM失败并非源于模型缺陷，而是未定决策请求结构本身导致的必然结果。

Abstract: The CAP theorem states that a distributed system cannot simultaneously guarantee consistency, availability, and partition tolerance under network partition. Inspired by this result, this paper formulates a CAP-like conjecture for Large Language Models (LLMs). The proposed trilemma states that, under semantic underdetermination, an LLM cannot always simultaneously guarantee strong correctness, strict non-bias, and high utility. A prompt is semantically underdetermined when the given premises do not determine a unique answer. In such cases, a useful and decisive response requires the model to introduce a selection criterion, preference, prior, or value ordering. If this criterion is not supplied by the user or justified by the available premises, the response becomes biased in a broad selection-theoretic sense. Conversely, if the model avoids unsupported preferences, it may preserve correctness and non-bias but may reduce utility through refusal, hedging, or clarification. The paper formalizes this correctness--non-bias--utility trilemma, develops examples, and argues that certain LLM failures arise not merely from model limitations but from the structure of underdetermined decision requests.

</details>


### [132] [OOM-Free Alpamayo via CPU-GPU Memory Swapping for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11678)
*Seungwoo Roh,Huiyeong Kim,Jong-Chan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需修改模型的系统级优化框架，可在显存受限的GPU上高效实现端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的推理，通过分层调度与性能预测，在保持精度的同时显著提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型需要20-60GB GPU显存，远超消费级GPU的12-16GB容量，限制了其在实际场景中的部署。

Method: 提出三阶段优化框架：顺序需求分层、流水线需求分层和GPU驻留层决策策略，并结合性能预测模型确定最优配置。

Result: 在RTX 5070Ti（16GB）上对Alpamayo-R1-10B模型实现了最高3.55倍于Accelerate卸载的速度提升，且保持完整BF16精度，预测误差低于1.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法仅通过系统级优化即可在资源受限设备上高效运行大型VLA模型，为自动驾驶的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving unify perception, reasoning, and control in a single neural network, achieving strong driving performance but requiring 20-60GB of GPU memory-far exceeding the 12-16GB available on commodity GPUs. We present a framework, which enables memory-efficient VLA inference on VRAM-constrained GPUs through system-level optimization alone, without model modification. Our work proceeds in three stages: (1) Sequential Demand Layering reduces VRAM usage from model-level to layer-level granularity; (2) Pipelined Demand Layering hides parameter transfer time within layer execution time via transfer--compute overlap; and (3) a GPU-Resident Layer Decision Policy, informed by per-module residency benefit analysis, eliminates the residual transfer overhead that pipelining cannot hide. We further propose a performance prediction model that determines the optimal configuration-both the number and placement of resident layers-from a single profiling run with less than 1.3% prediction error across all configurations. Applied to NVIDIA's Alpamayo-R1-10B (21.52GB) on an RTX 5070Ti (16GB), our work achieves up to 3.55x speedup over Accelerate offloading while maintaining full BF16 precision.

</details>


### [133] [Explaining and Breaking the Safety-Helpfulness Ceiling via Preference Dimensional Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11679)
*ShiYing Huang,Liang Lin,Yuer Li,Kaiwen Luo,Zhenhong Zhou,An Zhang,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Zhigang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MORA的新方法，通过多维奖励整合来解决大语言模型在多目标对齐中的零和冲突问题，实验表明其在顺序和同时对齐场景下均能显著提升各维度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标对齐方法通常只能在固定的帕累托前沿上进行权衡，无法根本缓解不同目标之间的冲突，因此需要一种能够突破提示限制、主动扩展多维奖励潜力的新方法。

Method: MORA方法通过预采样分离单奖励提示，并重写原始问题以融入多维意图，从而扩展奖励多样性；其核心在于分析提示对多维奖励的限制并加以优化。

Result: 在顺序对齐中，MORA在多个偏好对齐后使单一偏好指标提升了5%到12.4%，尤其在无害性方面增益显著；在同时对齐中，平均总体奖励提升了4.6%。

Conclusion: MORA通过重构提示以增强多维奖励表达，有效缓解了多目标对齐中的零和冲突，突破了传统方法的帕累托限制，为多目标优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: In the realm of multi-objective alignment for large language models, balancing disparate human preferences often manifests as a zero-sum conflict. Specifically, the intrinsic tension between competing goals dictates that aggressively optimizing for one metric (e.g., helpfulness) frequently incurs a substantial penalty on another (e.g., harmlessness). While prior work mainly focuses on data selection, parameter merging, or algorithmic balancing during training, these approaches merely force compromises between divergent preferences along a fixed Pareto frontier, failing to fundamentally resolve the inherent trade-off. In this work, we approach this problem from a novel perspective of multi-dimensional rewards. By scaling up the model's rollouts and analyzing the outputs across different reward dimensions, we arrive at a critical conclusion: the conflict among multiple objectives stems from the fact that the prompt itself inherently restricts the achievable multi-dimensional rewards. Based on this core observation, we propose MORA: Multi-Objective Reward Assimilation. Specifically, MORA isolates single-reward prompts through pre-sampling and expands their reward diversity by rewriting the original questions to incorporate multi-dimensional intents. Extensive experiments demonstrate that: (1) in sequential alignment, MORA achieves single-preference improvements ranging from 5% to 12.4%, with exceptional gains in harmlessness, after multiple-preference alignment across helpful, harmless, and truthful dimensions. (2) In simultaneous alignment, MORA achieves an average overall reward improvement of 4.6%. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MORA-MPA.

</details>


### [134] [Persistent and Conversational Multi-Method Explainability for Trustworthy Financial AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11687)
*Georgios Makridis,Georgios Fatouros,John Soldatos,George Katsis,Dimosthenis Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向金融情感分析的人本可解释AI架构，通过将多种XAI方法的结果持久化存储、支持跨方法三角验证，并结合检索增强生成对话系统，提升解释的可信度与可用性。实验表明，约束性提示显著降低了幻觉率并提高了方法引用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域对AI解释的可靠性、一致性和可访问性要求日益提高，现有XAI方法缺乏持久化管理、多方法验证和人机交互支持，难以满足监管和决策需求。

Method: 1）将LIME、遮蔽重要性评分和显著性热图等XAI结果以结构化元数据形式存入S3存储；2）构建基于RAG的对话助手，实现多方法解释的比较与自然语言合成；3）通过自动化检查评估解释的完整性、幻觉和方法归属准确性。

Result: 在FinBERT基础上构建的EXTRA-BRAIN管道中验证了该架构，结果显示约束提示使幻觉率降低36%，方法引用增加73%。

Conclusion: 该架构提升了金融AI系统的可解释性、鲁棒性和人机协作能力，为监管环境下的可信AI服务提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Financial institutions increasingly require AI explanations that are persistent, cross-validated across methods, and conversationally accessible to human decision-makers. We present an architecture for human-centered explainable AI in financial sentiment analysis that combines three contributions. First, we treat XAI artifacts -- LIME feature attributions, occlusion-based word importance scores, and saliency heatmaps -- as persistent, searchable objects in distributed S3-compatible storage with structured metadata and natural-language summaries, enabling semantic retrieval over explanation history and automatic index reconstruction after system failures. Second, we enable multi-method explanation triangulation, where a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) assistant compares and synthesizes results from multiple XAI methods applied to the same prediction, allowing users to assess explanation robustness through natural-language dialogue. Third, we evaluate the faithfulness of generated explanations using automated checks over grounding completeness, hallucinated claims, and method-attribution behavior. We demonstrate the architecture on an EXTRA-BRAIN financial sentiment analysis pipeline using FinBERT predictions and present evaluation results showing that constrained prompting reduces hallucination rate by 36\% and increases method-attribution citations by 73\% compared to naive prompting. We discuss implications for trustworthy, human-centered AI services in regulated financial environments.

</details>


### [135] [Toward Stable Value Alignment: Introducing Independent Modules for Consistent Value Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11712)
*Wenhao Chen,Sirui Sun,Shengyuan Bai,Guojie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SVGT的新方法，通过独立的价值模块和显式的生成引导，在不干扰主干模型的情况下实现大语言模型与人类价值观的稳定对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐方法因依赖动态且脆弱的残差流，难以稳定表达人类价值观，缺乏对价值表征稳定性的建模。

Method: 设计一个独立的价值模块，包括独立的价值空间建模和将稳定信号转化为可学习的Bridge Tokens以进行显式行为引导。

Result: 在多个主干模型和安全基准上的实验表明，SVGT平均降低超过70%的有害内容生成，同时保持生成流畅性。

Conclusion: SVGT通过架构层面的价值建模，实现了跨不同上下文的鲁棒价值对齐，验证了分离且显式价值引导的有效性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values typically relies on post-training or inference-time steering that directly manipulates the backbone's parameters or representation space. However, a critical gap exists: the model's residual stream is highly dynamic, in which values exist as fragile, low-dimensional properties, inherently incompatible with the stability required for consistent value expression. In this paper, we propose the Stable Value Guidance Transformer (SVGT), which addresses this gap through an independent value module incorporating two key designs: (1) independent value modeling, maintaining normative representations in a dedicated value space isolated from the backbone, and (2) explicit behavioral guidance, transducing these stable signals into learnable latent Bridge Tokens. These tokens serve as dynamic value anchors to explicitly steer the generative trajectory, ensuring robust adherence across diverse contexts without disrupting the backbone's internal representations. Experiments across multiple backbones and safety benchmarks show that SVGT generally reduces harmful scores by over 70% while maintaining generation fluency, demonstrating the efficacy of architecturally grounded value modeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/Clervils/SVGT.git.

</details>


### [136] [SafeSteer: A Decoding-level Defense Mechanism for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11716)
*Xinyi Zeng,Xue Yang,Jingyuan Zhang,Huanqian Yan,Xiang Chen,Kaiwen Wei,Hankun Kang,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SafeSteer的解码级防御机制，用于提升多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全性，无需微调即可有效识别和纠正有害输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于MLLMs输入特征的异构性，现有防御方法依赖昂贵的微调或低效的事后干预，难以应对新型攻击且存在性能权衡，因此需要一种更高效、灵活的防御方法。

Method: 通过分析MLLMs在解码阶段辨别有害性的内在能力，设计了一个轻量级的Decoding-Probe，在解码过程中动态检测并纠偏；同时引入模态语义对齐向量，将文本模态的强安全性迁移到视觉模态。

Result: 在多个MLLM上实验表明，SafeSteer最多可将模型安全性提升33.40%，且无需微调，同时保持了模型的有用性与无害性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: SafeSteer是一种高效的解码级防御方法，能够充分利用MLLMs的内在安全能力，有效抵御包括图像攻击在内的多种攻击，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are gaining increasing attention. Due to the heterogeneity of their input features, they face significant challenges in terms of jailbreak defenses. Current defense methods rely on costly fine-tuning or inefficient post-hoc interventions, limiting their ability to address novel attacks and involving performance trade-offs. To address the above issues, we explore the inherent safety capabilities within MLLMs and quantify their intrinsic ability to discern harmfulness at decoding stage. We observe that 1) MLLMs can distinguish the harmful and harmless inputs during decoding process, 2) Image-based attacks are more stealthy. Based on these insights, we introduce SafeSteer, a decoding-level defense mechanism for MLLMs. Specifically, it includes a Decoding-Probe, a lightweight probe for detecting and correcting harmful output during decoding, which iteratively steers the decoding process toward safety. Furthermore, a modal semantic alignment vector is integrated to transfer the strong textual safety alignment to the vision modality. Experiments on multiple MLLMs demonstrate that SafeSterr can improve MLLMs' safety by up to 33.40\% without fine-tuning. Notably, it can maintain the effectiveness of MLLMs, ensuring a balance between their helpfulness and harmlessness.

</details>


### [137] [OptArgus: A Multi-Agent System to Detect Hallucinations in LLM-based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11738)
*Zhong Li,Zihan Guo,Xiaohan Lu,Juntao Wang,Jie Song,Chao Shen,Jiageng Wu,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了优化建模中的“幻觉检测”问题，提出首个细粒度的幻觉分类体系，并设计了多智能体检测系统OptArgus，在自建基准上验证了其在减少误报、准确定位和自然输出检测上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在将自然语言优化问题转化为数学建模时可能产生语义偏差，仅依赖目标值匹配无法确保正确性，因此需要结构一致性审计来检测优化建模中的幻觉现象。

Method: 构建了一个针对优化建模的细粒度幻觉分类体系，基于该体系设计了多智能体检测系统OptArgus，包含导引模块、专业审计员和证据整合机制，并在包含人工构造与LLM生成样本的基准套件上进行评估。

Result: OptArgus相比单智能体基线在干净样本上误报更少，在单错误控制样本中定位更准确，在自然生成结果中检测能力更强。

Conclusion: 将优化建模幻觉检测转化为可实证的研究问题，表明基于分类体系的模块化审计是提升建模可靠性的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to translate natural-language optimization problems into mathematical formulations and solver code, but matching the reference objective value is not a reliable test of correctness: an artifact may agree numerically while still changing the underlying optimization semantics. We formulate this issue as \emph{optimization-modeling hallucination detection}, namely structural consistency auditing over the problem description, symbolic model, and solver implementation. We develop, to our knowledge, the first fine-grained hallucination taxonomy specifically for optimization modeling, spanning objective, variable, constraint, and implementation failures. We use this taxonomy to design OptArgus, a multi-agent detector with conductor routing, specialist auditors, and evidence consolidation. To evaluate this setting, we introduce a three-part benchmark suite with $484$ clean artifacts, $1266$ controlled injected artifacts, and $6292$ natural LLM-generated artifacts. Against a matched single-agent baseline, OptArgus produces fewer false alarms on clean artifacts, more accurate top-ranked localization on controlled single-error cases, and stronger detection on natural model outputs. Together, these contributions turn optimization-modeling hallucination detection into a concrete empirical problem and suggest that modular, taxonomy-grounded auditing is a practical route to more reliable optimization modeling.

</details>


### [138] [Towards Visually Grounded Multimodal Summarization via Cross-Modal Transformer and Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11753)
*Abid Ali,Diego Molla-Aliod,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多模态摘要的统一框架SPeCTrA-Sum，通过深度视觉处理器实现文本与视觉的层次化融合，并利用轻量级视觉相关性预测器选择代表性图像，提升了摘要的准确性和视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多模态摘要中通常将浅层视觉特征注入深层语言模型，导致表示不匹配和跨模态对齐弱的问题。为此，本文旨在解决视觉与文本表示之间的层级不一致，并增强图文语义对齐。

Method: 提出SPeCTrA-Sum框架，包含两个关键组件：1）深度视觉处理器（DVP），在对应深度上对齐视觉编码器与语言模型，实现层次化的逐层融合；2）轻量级视觉相关性预测器（VRP），通过从DPP教师模型蒸馏软标签来选择显著且多样化的图像。模型采用结合自回归摘要、跨模态对齐和DPP蒸馏的多目标损失进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，该系统生成的摘要更准确、更具视觉依据，同时能选出更具代表性的图像，在多模态摘要任务中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 深度感知的跨模态融合与基于DPP的图像选择策略有效提升了多模态摘要的质量，验证了层次对齐和原则性图像选择的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal summarization requires models to jointly understand textual and visual inputs to generate concise, semantically coherent summaries. Existing methods often inject shallow visual features into deep language models, leading to representational mismatches and weak cross-modal grounding. We propose a unified framework that jointly performs text summarization and representative image selection. Our system, SPeCTrA-Sum (Sampler Perceiver with Cross-modal Transformer and gated Attention for Summarization), introduces two key innovations. First, a Deep Visual Processor (DVP) aligns the visual encoder with the language model at corresponding depths, enabling hierarchical, layer-wise fusion that preserves semantic consistency. Second, a lightweight Visual Relevance Predictor (VRP) selects salient and diverse images by distilling soft labels from a Determinantal Point Processes (DPP) teacher. SPeCTrA-Sum is trained using a multi-objective loss that combines autoregressive summarization, cross-modal alignment, and DPP-based distillation. Experiments show that our system produces more accurate, visually grounded summaries and selects more representative images, demonstrating the benefits of depth-aware fusion and principled image selection for multimodal summarization.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [ECHO: Continuous Hierarchical Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10993)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jiayi Lu,Ruixuan Yang,Jun Ye,Boran Zhao,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ECHO的新型记忆框架，通过在连续层次空间中组织视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的经验，提升了长周期操作任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强架构缺乏对操作类别和层次结构的先验知识，导致经验检索效率低且难以泛化到未见过的长周期任务组合。

Method: 采用双曲自编码器将VLA隐藏状态映射到连续层次空间，并利用双曲度量和蕴含约束机制构建语义记忆树，同时通过几何插值和结构分裂进行背景记忆巩固。

Result: 在LIBERO数据集和初步真实世界实验中，ECHO相比π₀基线在LIBERO-Long上执行成功率绝对提升了12.8%，并在跨套件未见任务上表现出更好的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: ECHO通过层次化记忆组织和持续巩固机制，有效提升了VLA模型在长周期任务中的记忆利用效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Memory capacity is a critical factor determining the performance of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in long-horizon manipulation tasks. Existing memory-augmented architectures primarily rely on linear or flat storage, lacking structural priors for manipulation categories and hierarchical organization. This deficiency hinders efficient experience retrieval and limits generalization to unseen long-horizon task compositions. Inspired by the hierarchical organization of human experience, we propose ECHO (Experience Consolidation and Hierarchical Organization), a novel memory framework operating within a Continuous Hierarchical Space. By employing a hyperbolic autoencoder, ECHO maps VLA hidden states into this space. Leveraging hyperbolic metrics and entailment constraint mechanisms, experience vectors are organized into a semantic memory tree that supports efficient top-down retrieval. In parallel, a background consolidation mechanism continuously refines the memory tree through geometric interpolation and structural splitting, supporting virtual memory synthesis in the continuous space. We integrate ECHO into the $π_0$ foundation model. Evaluations on LIBERO and preliminary real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, notably achieving a 12.8% absolute improvement in execution success rate over the $π_0$ baseline on LIBERO-Long, while improving compositional generalization on cross-suite unseen long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [140] [ForceFlow: Learning to Feel and Act via Contact-Driven Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11048)
*Shuoheng Zhang,Yifu Yuan,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Qiaojun Yu,Pengyi Li,Guowei Huang,Helong Huang,Xingyue Quan,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ForceFlow的力感知反应式框架，用于机器人在接触丰富的操作任务中实现更优的模仿学习。该框架通过流匹配建模，并采用不对称多模态融合结构，将力信号作为全局调节信号，结合视觉与力觉信息分阶段控制，显著提升了成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法在处理需要高精度力反馈的复杂接触操作任务时仍面临挑战，尤其是在多模态感知下实现鲁棒泛化仍缺乏简单有效的框架。

Method: 提出ForceFlow框架：1）在接触阶段策略中引入不对称多模态融合结构，将力信号作为全局调节信号，并结合即时与历史力信息进行联合预测；2）在任务层级上采用视觉主导的接近阶段与触觉主导的交互阶段解耦设计，通过VLM实现目标定位，力控执行接触操作，并引入Vision-to-Force（V2F）交接机制。

Result: 在六个真实世界的高接触性任务中，ForceFlow相比强基线ForceVLA提升了37%的成功率，具备更低的成本、准确的力信号预测能力，并在接触力自调节和零样本OOD泛化方面表现优越。

Conclusion: ForceFlow通过深度融合力觉与视觉信息、分阶段策略设计和高效的多模态融合架构，为接触丰富任务提供了一个高效、鲁棒且具强泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods enable robots to interact autonomously with the physical environment. However, contact-rich manipulation tasks remain a significant challenge due to complex contact dynamics that demand high-precision force feedback and control. Although recent efforts have attempted to integrate force/torque sensing into policies, how to build a simple yet effective framework that achieves robust generalization under multimodal observations remains an open question. In this paper, we propose ForceFlow, a force-aware reactive framework built upon flow matching. For contact-stage policy design, we investigate force signal fusion mechanisms and adopt an asymmetric multimodal fusion architecture that treats force as a global regulatory signal, combined with a joint prediction paradigm that enhances the policy's understanding of instantaneous force and historical information, thereby achieving deep coupling between force and motion. For task-level hierarchical decomposition, we divide manipulation into a vision-dominant approach stage (VLM-based pointing for target localization) and a touch-dominant interaction stage (force-driven contact execution), with a Vision-to-Force (V2F) handover mechanism that explicitly decouples spatial generalization from contact regulation. Experimental results across six real-world contact-rich tasks demonstrate that ForceFlow achieves a 37% success rate improvement over the strong baseline ForceVLA while maintaining significantly lower cost. Moreover, ForceFlow exhibits accurate force signal prediction and demonstrates superior performance in contact force self-regulation and zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

</details>


### [141] [SEVO: Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation for Robust VLA Manipulation via Active Illumination and Data-Centric Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11114)
*Tianchonghui Fang,Yuan Zhuang,Fei Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEVO是一种数据驱动的方法，通过改进观察设计和数据采集多样性来提升低成本机器人在新环境中操作的泛化能力，显著提高了视觉-语言-动作策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和模仿学习策略在训练环境外部署时表现差，社区报告跨环境迁移成功率接近零，而传统基准测试高估了实际性能，因此需要更有效的跨环境鲁棒性解决方案。

Method: 提出SEVO方法，结合三类改进：体固定摄像头覆盖全工作区、主动红光照明标准化物体外观、实时YOLO分割提供语义线索，并强调多样化数据采集（变化光照、背景、干扰物）对泛化的关键作用。

Result: 在训练环境中，SEVO使ACT和SmolVLA分别达到95%和83%的抓取成功率，在新环境中仍保持85%和75%；无SEVO时，训练环境为75%/70%，新环境骤降至30-35%。

Conclusion: 观察设计原则和数据采集多样性比模型扩展更能有效提升低成本机器人在日常家庭环境中的可靠性，是实现良好跨环境迁移的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and imitation-learning policies trained via community toolchains on low-cost hardware frequently fail when deployed outside the training environment. Existing evaluations, including the original ACT and SmolVLA benchmarks, demonstrate high success rates under controlled, fixed backgrounds, yet community practitioners report near-zero transfer to new environments. We present SEVO (Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation), a data-centric approach that improves cross-environment manipulation robustness without modifying the policy architecture. SEVO transforms the raw RGB camera stream through three mechanisms: (1) body-fixed cameras whose combined fields of view cover the full manipulation workspace, (2) active red-spectrum illumination that physically normalizes object appearance, and (3) real-time YOLO segmentation overlay that provides a background-invariant semantic cue. Critically, we show that a diversified data collection protocol (systematically varying lighting, backgrounds, and distractors during teleoperation) is the single most important factor for generalization. We target transparent water bottles, objects that visually blend with their surroundings, and select a simple pick-and-place task to enable hundreds of controlled real-robot trials across two mobile platforms. The full pipeline achieves 95% grasp success with ACT and 83% with SmolVLA in the training environment, transferring to novel environments at 85% and 75%. Without SEVO, the same policies achieve only 75%/70% in training and collapse to 30-35% in novel environments. Our results demonstrate that principled observation design and environmental diversity during data collection, not model scaling, enable low-cost robots to operate reliably in everyday household environments.

</details>


### [142] [ASIP-Planner: Adaptive Planning for UAV Surface Inspection in Partially Known Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11119)
*Hanyu Jin,Zhefan Xu,Haoyu Shen,Xinming Han,Kanlong Ye,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于部分已知结构化室内环境的自适应无人机检测框架，结合基于分段的全局覆盖规划器与面向检测的局部视角自适应模块，提高了在存在未预见障碍物情况下的检测覆盖率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有覆盖规划方法在环境变化时易受先验地图不准确影响的问题，特别是在室内检测中由于临时结构或设备导致视野遮挡的情况。

Method: 提出一种融合全局覆盖规划与局部视角调整的框架：全局规划器将平面检测目标组织为表面对齐的簇以生成紧凑且方向一致的观测序列；局部规划器在线生成无碰撞轨迹并动态调整观测方向以减少遮挡导致的覆盖损失。

Result: 仿真结果显示该方法在随机场景下实现了接近完整的覆盖，并缩短了轨迹长度；真实飞行实验验证了其能产生可用于下游分析的有效检测数据。

Conclusion: 所提框架显著提升了在部分已知室内环境中无人机检测的适应性和效率，适用于基于不完整先验信息的实际检测任务。

Abstract: Indoor infrastructure inspection, such as tunnels and industrial facilities, requires systematic surface coverage to ensure that all inspection targets are properly observed. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer an alternative to manual inspection by conducting map-guided surface inspection using prior structural models. However, in practice, indoor inspection often relies on floorplan-derived reference maps that may not reflect unforeseen obstacles, such as temporary structures or equipment, leading to occluded viewpoints and degraded inspection quality. Existing coverage planning methods typically assume a fully known inspection environment and perform deterministic global viewpoint optimization based on accurate prior maps, making them vulnerable to environmental discrepancies during execution. This work presents an adaptive UAV inspection framework for partially known structured indoor environments. The proposed method integrates a segment-based global coverage planner with an inspection-oriented local view-angle adaptation module. The global planner organizes planar inspection targets into surface-aligned clusters to generate compact viewpoint sequences with improved orientation consistency. The local planner generates collision-free trajectories and adjusts the viewing direction online to mitigate occlusion-induced coverage loss while preserving the planned trajectory structure. The simulation results across randomized scene configurations demonstrate that the proposed global planner achieves near-complete coverage while reducing trajectory length compared to representative baselines. Real-world flight experiments further validate that the framework produces usable inspection data for downstream analysis. These results indicate that the proposed framework improves inspection efficiency and adaptability in partially known structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [143] [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144)
*Kaixin Jia,Jiacheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Forecast-GS 是一种语言条件下的机器人操作预测性3D表示框架，通过显式预测任务完成状态来提升动作评估的可靠性，在真实世界的抓取放置任务中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作系统通常仅基于当前场景进行推理，缺乏对任务完成状态的显式建模，导致在部分观测下难以判断动作是否能达成目标。

Method: 提出 Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS)，将语言指令与3D场景预测结合，构建可解释的从语言理解到操作规划的桥梁，并在真实机器人平台上验证。

Result: 在 Cutter-to-Box、Apple-to-Bowl 和 Sponge-to-Tray 任务中，Forecast-GS 自动选择的成功率分别为 21/25、23/25 和 16/25，优于 ReKep 基线；人工辅助下进一步提升至 23/25、24/25 和 19/25。

Conclusion: 显式预测任务完成状态有助于更可靠的动作评估，但自动排序仍存在挑战，Forecast-GS 为语言、感知与操作规划之间提供了可解释的连接。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS), a predictive 3D representation framework for language-conditioned robotic manipulation. While recent manipulation systems have made progress by grounding language instructions into robot affordances, value maps, or relational keypoint constraints, they usually reason over the current scene and do not explicitly model the task-completed state. This limitation is critical when success depends on satisfying spatial and semantic goals under partial observations, where the robot must evaluate whether a candidate action leads to a feasible task-consistent outcome.
  We validate Forecast-GS on real-world pick-and-place manipulation tasks, including Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, and Sponge-to-Tray. For each task, we conduct 25 real-world trials under varied initial object configurations using the same robot platform and sensing setup. Forecast-GS with automatic candidate selection achieves success rates of 21/25, 23/25, and 16/25 on the three tasks, respectively, outperforming the ReKep baseline, which achieves 15/25, 19/25, and 10/25. A diagnostic human-assisted setting further improves success rates to 23/25, 24/25, and 19/25, suggesting that candidate generation is effective while automatic ranking remains imperfect. These results suggest that explicitly forecasting task-completed 3D states enables more reliable action evaluation, while the gap between automatic and human-assisted selection indicates that robust final-state ranking remains an important challenge for fully autonomous manipulation. Overall, Forecast-GS provides an interpretable bridge between language understanding, 3D perception, and robotic manipulation planning.

</details>


### [144] [Distributed Pose Graph Optimization via Continuous Riemannian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11210)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于李群上二阶连续时间动力系统的分布式位姿图优化框架，通过阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程和几何积分器设计优化算法，在多机器人系统中实现高效、低通信开销的并行求解，并在理论和实验上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决分布式位姿图优化（PGO）中的通信开销大、收敛慢以及异步环境下性能下降的问题，需要一种能够有效建模状态与速度、支持延迟鲁棒性的新方法。

Method: 将PGO问题建模为李群上的二阶连续时间动力系统，视位姿变量为受阻尼作用的质点；采用阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程作为控制方程，并结合半隐式几何积分器设计优化算法；在多机器人场景中使用块对角质量与阻尼矩阵实现完全分布式的并行计算，利用状态与速度建模进行邻居运动预测以提升异步条件下的收敛性。

Result: 理论分析证明了所用几何离散化方案下能量耗散的充分条件；实验表明该方法在同步与异步模式下均优于现有最先进分布式基线方法，具有更优的收敛性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于物理启发的二阶动力系统方法为分布式PGO提供了一个统一且高效的求解框架，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势，特别适用于存在通信延迟的多机器人协同定位任务。

Abstract: We present a framework for distributed Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) by formulating the problem as a second-order continuous-time dynamical system evolving on Lie groups. By modeling pose variables as massive particles subject to damping, the equilibrium points of the resulting Riemannian dynamics coincide with first-order critical points of the original PGO problem. Using the governing damped Euler--Poincaré equations and a semi-implicit geometric integrator, we design an optimization algorithm that generalizes existing algorithms such as Riemannian gradient descent and Gauss--Newton. In multi-robot settings, we present a fully distributed and parallel method based on block-diagonal mass and damping matrices, where each robot solves an ordinary differential equation for its own poses with minimal communication overhead. Moreover, modeling both state and velocity enables principled neighbor prediction that significantly improves convergence under delayed communication. Theoretically, we present an analysis and establish sufficient condition that ensures energy dissipation under the employed geometric discretization scheme. Experiments on benchmark PGO datasets demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art distributed baselines in both synchronous and asynchronous regimes.

</details>


### [145] [Computational Design of a Low-Visibility UAV Using a Human-Aligned Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11296)
*Jingxian Wang,Chen Yu,David Matthews,Emma Alexander,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Phantom Twist是一种单螺旋桨无人机，通过高速旋转和运动模糊实现低可见性，采用两阶段自动化设计流程优化组件布局，在满足飞行稳定性的前提下显著降低视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现无人机在飞行过程中的低可视性，提升隐蔽性，避免被轻易察觉。

Method: 提出一种名为Phantom Twist的单螺旋桨无人机，开发了两阶段自动化设计流程，利用人类对感知的度量标准（LPIPS）最小化可见性，并满足惯性和空气动力学约束。

Result: 通过制造和飞行测试多个原型验证了该方法的有效性，结果显示优化后的无人机在保持稳定可控的同时，视觉可探测性显著低于传统四旋翼无人机。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了通过结构设计与运动模糊相结合来降低无人机视觉可见性的目标，为隐蔽无人机设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Phantom Twist, a type of single-propeller UAV designed to achieve low visibility through high-speed spinning and the exploitation of motion blur. We develop a two-stage automated design pipeline that optimizes the placement of functional components including batteries, control PCB, motor-propeller assembly, and counterweights. The pipeline minimizes visibility as measured by a human-aligned perceptual metric (LPIPS) while strictly satisfying inertial and aerodynamic constraints required for stable flight. We validate this approach through fabrication and flight testing of multiple prototypes. These tests confirm that our pipeline produces stable, controllable designs and that the optimized UAV exhibits significantly reduced visual perceptibility compared to conventional quadcopters.

</details>


### [146] [Kairos: A Scalable Serving System for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11381)
*Yinwei Dai,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Landon Cox,Xenofon Foukas,Bozidar Radunovic,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kairos是一种专为物理AI设计的多机器人服务系统，通过将生成-执行循环作为核心机制，显著降低了任务端到端延迟，且性能增益随机器人规模扩大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字AI服务系统无法有效支持物理AI中推理与执行交替进行的多轮生成-执行模式，限制了其在大规模机器人部署中的应用。

Method: 设计Kairos系统，将生成-执行循环作为一等公民，主动参与执行阶段，并针对物理AI的工作负载特性进行优化。

Result: 在多种物理AI模型和机器人上测试表明，相比最先进的数字AI服务方法，Kairos平均端到端任务延迟降低了31.8%至66.5%，且增益随机器人队列规模增大而增加。

Conclusion: Kairos是首个适应物理AI特性的多机器人服务系统，有效提升了大规模机器人场景下的服务效率，推动了物理AI的实际部署。

Abstract: Physical AI is experiencing rapid growth with frontier foundation models increasing its capabilities across general environments. Physical AI tasks are characterized by inference properties that are markedly different from digital AI. They consist of multiple rounds of inference and action execution, generating a chunk of actions in each inference round, and asynchronously interleaving inference and execution. This makes existing digital AI serving systems unsuited for physical AI; a shortcoming that is critical for enabling their wide adoption, considering their size and the scale of the robot fleets they have to serve. To fill this gap, we design Kairos, the first multi-robot serving system that makes the generate-execute loop a first-class citizen, with active involvement in the execution phase. Across a wide range of physical AI models and robots, Kairos reduces the average end-to-end task latency by 31.8--66.5% over state-of-the-art digital AI serving practices, with gains scaling with the robot fleet size.

</details>


### [147] [Overcoming Dynamics-Blindness: Training-Free Pace-and-Path Correction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11459)
*Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Vikash Singh,Xinpeng Li,Kai Ye,Zhe Hu,Zhongzhu Pu,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的推理时操作符“节奏与路径校正”（Pace-and-Path Correction），用于提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在动态环境中的表现，通过联合优化节奏和路径通道，在不需重新训练的情况下显著提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多基于单帧观测，缺乏对时间动态的感知能力，在非平稳场景中性能严重下降；现有改进方法存在高成本重训练或延迟高、时序不一致等问题。

Method: 提出“节奏与路径校正”方法，作为一种训练免费、闭式解的推理时操作符，适用于任何分块动作VLA模型；通过单一二次代价函数的联合最小化，将解正交分解为两个通道：节奏通道压缩执行方向上的进度，路径通道施加正交空间偏移，共同吸收块窗口内的动态感知误差。

Result: 在专门设计的动态诊断基准MoveBench上进行评估，结果表明该方法优于现有的训练免费包装器和动态自适应方法，在仅动态和静态-动态混合环境中，相对于基础VLA模型绝对成功率分别提升高达28.8%和25.9%。

Conclusion: Pace-and-Path Correction是一种高效、无需训练的推理时修正机制，能够有效增强VLA模型对时间动态的鲁棒性，显著提升其在动态环境中的执行性能，具有广泛适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve remarkable flexibility and generalization beyond classical control paradigms. However, most prevailing VLAs are trained under a single-frame observation paradigm, which leaves them structurally blind to temporal dynamics. Consequently, these models degrade severely in non-stationary scenarios, even when trained or finetuned on dynamic datasets. Existing approaches either require expensive retraining or suffer from latency bottlenecks and poor temporal consistency across action chunks. We propose Pace-and-Path Correction, a training-free, closed-form inference-time operator that wraps any chunked-action VLA. From a single quadratic cost, joint minimization yields a unified solution that decomposes orthogonally into two distinct channels. The pace channel compresses execution along the planned direction, while the path channel applies an orthogonal spatial offset, jointly absorbing the perceived dynamics within the chunk window. We evaluate our approach on a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark MoveBench designed to isolate motion as the sole controlled variable. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free wrappers and dynamic-adaptive methods and improves success rates by up to 28.8% and 25.9% in absolute terms over foundational VLA models in dynamic-only and static-dynamic mixed environments, respectively.

</details>


### [148] [Offline Policy Evaluation for Manipulation Policies via Discounted Liveness Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11479)
*Hao Wang,Joshua Bowden,Colton Crosby,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于活性（liveness）的贝尔曼算子，用于在有限时域和稀疏奖励下进行机器人策略的离线评估，有效缓解了截断偏差并更准确地反映任务进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现代操作系统的奖励稀疏、轨迹非单调且执行长度有限，传统基于无限时域假设的策略评估方法存在截断偏差，难以准确评估策略性能。

Method: 提出一种基于活性的贝尔曼算子，将策略评估视为任务完成问题，构建对截断鲁棒的保守值函数，并提供理论分析，包括收敛性和任务进展建模能力。

Result: 在模拟操作任务和真实布料折叠任务中验证了方法的有效性，相比TD(0)和蒙特卡洛等经典方法，能更准确反映任务进展并显著降低截断偏差。

Conclusion: 所提方法为有限时域、稀疏奖励下的机器人策略离线评估提供了理论可靠且实践有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Policy evaluation is a fundamental component of the development and deployment pipeline for robotic policies. In modern manipulation systems, this problem is particularly challenging: rewards are often sparse, task progression of evaluation rollouts are often non-monotonic as the policies exhibit recovery behaviors, and evaluation rollouts are necessarily of finite length. This finite length introduces truncation bias, breaking the infinite-horizon assumptions underlying standard methods relying on Bellman equations/principle of optimality. In this work, we propose a framework for offline policy evaluation from sparse rewards based on a liveness-based Bellman operator. Our formulation interprets policy evaluation as a task-completion problem and yields a conservative fixed-point value function that is robust to finite-horizon truncation. We analyze the theoretical properties of the proposed operator, including contraction guarantees, and show how it encodes task progression while mitigating truncation bias.
